## Extension List/Browse Page: Complete Codebase Mapping

Based on a thorough analysis of the AgentCenter Nuxt 4 marketplace, here's a structured inventory of the extension list page and its surrounding infrastructure:

---

### 1. THE EXTENSION LIST PAGE

**File:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/extensions/index.vue` (lines 1–64)

**Page structure:**
- Locale-prefixed route: `/en/extensions` or `/zh/extensions` (via `@nuxtjs/i18n` prefix strategy)
- Fetches two endpoints in parallel:
  - `/api/internal/extensions` — list items + total count (filtered via `route.query`)
  - `/api/internal/facets` — filter facets (creators, publishers, tags) — fetched once and reused across navigations
- Page layout:
  - **Header** (lines 33–50): Title ("Browse Extensions"), total count, and a conditional "MCP Panorama" link (shows only when `category=mcp` filter is active)
  - **FilterBar component** (lines 52–56): Passes facets to filter controls
  - **ExtGrid component** (lines 58–62): Renders the extension card grid or empty state
  - Full-width container: `max-w-7xl mx-auto` with `px-6 py-8`

**Key computed properties:**
- `items` — the extension list from `data.value?.items`
- `total` — count for display
- `query` — current search query from filters
- `filtersActive` — boolean to show/hide "Clear Filters" button in empty state
- `isMcpCategory` — conditional for "View Panorama" banner

---

### 2. EXTENSION CARD GRID & CARD COMPONENTS

#### **ExtGrid** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/extension/ExtGrid.vue` (lines 1–44)

**Renders:**
- Grid layout: `grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))] gap-4` — responsive auto-fill with 280px min card width
- **Empty state** (lines 18–37) shows when `items.length === 0`:
  - Icon: `SearchX` from lucide-vue-next
  - Conditional messaging based on context: query, filtered state, or no extensions yet
  - "Clear Filters" link (if filters are active)
  - Styled with dashed border, `bg-(--color-card)/40`, and centered flex column
- **Card loop** (line 42): `v-for="ext in items"` → renders `ExtCard` per item

---

#### **ExtCard** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue` (lines 1–128)

**Layout (as `<article>`)**
- Border, rounded card: `rounded-(--radius-card)` (10px), border on hover lift effect: `hover:-translate-y-0.5 hover:border-(--color-accent)/30`
- **Header section** (lines 53–92):
  - Icon emoji in a colored box (background + border computed from `ext.iconColor`)
  - Extension name (with `.after:absolute` for whole-card click targeting)
  - Badge (Official, Popular, New) — styled with `.badge-{official|popular|new}` classes
  - Department tag (if `ext.deptId` exists): colored differently if user's own dept (uses accent tint)

- **Description** (lines 94–100):
  - 2-line clamp with `-webkit-box` truncation
  - Smaller text: `text-[12.5px]`

- **Tags** (lines 102–110):
  - Hash-prefixed monospace tags: `#{{ tagLabel(tag) }}`
  - Styled as bordered pills: `border border-(--color-border)` with monospace font

- **Footer metrics + Install** (lines 112–127):
  - Star rating (filled star icon, 5-point average)
  - Download count (icon + formatted count, e.g., "1.2k")
  - InstallButton component (size="sm", positioned `z-10` to stay above whole-card link)

**Computed properties:**
- `name`, `desc` — locale-aware (EN/ZH)
- `deptTrail`, `deptLeaf` — department breadcrumb
- `isMine` — check if extension belongs to user's dept
- `formatCount()` — abbreviates large numbers (1000+ → "1k", etc.)

---

### 3. FILTER COMPONENTS (under `app/components/filters/`)

All filters follow **Mode B (drawer-style multi-row)** per CLAUDE.md decision #3.

#### **FilterBar** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` (lines 1–24)

**Layout hierarchy:**
- Row 1: `ScopePills` + `DeptPicker` + `CreatorPicker` + `PublisherPicker` (inline flex, gap-3)
- Row 2: `TagDrawer` (full-width drawer below row 1)
- Row 3: `FilterChips` (inline pills) + `SortSelect` (dropdown, right-aligned `justify-between`)
- Border-bottom separator + padding

---

#### **ScopePills** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` (lines 1–39)

- 4 scope buttons: "All" + "Personal" + "Org" + "Enterprise"
- Toggle-style: active state = `bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-bg)`, inactive = neutral border
- No accent color per decision #11 (secondary active states use bold weight + border, not tint)
- Preceded by label: "Scope:"

---

- Dropdown content:

  - Selected state: bold weight, no accent tint
- Trigger styling when active: `border-(--color-ink)/35 bg-(--color-card)` (neutral ink accent)

### 5. DATA SHAPE & SERVER ENDPOINTS

#### **Extension List Item Type** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/shared/db/queries-types.ts` (lines 1–29)

```typescript
export interface ExtensionListItem {
  id: string
  slug: string
  category: ExtensionCategory  // "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins"
  badge: ExtensionBadge | null  // "official" | "popular" | "new"
  scope: ExtensionScope  // "personal" | "org" | "enterprise"
  funcCat: FuncCatKey | null  // "workTask" | "business" | "tools"
  subCat: string | null  // L1 taxonomy key
  l2: string | null  // L2 (leaf) taxonomy key
  deptId: string | null  // dotted-path dept ID (e.g. "eng.cloud.infra")
  iconEmoji: string | null
  iconColor: string | null  // hex color, e.g. "#FF6B6B"
  name: string
  nameZh: string | null
  tagline: string | null  // short description
  taglineZh: string | null
  description: string | null  // longer description
  descriptionZh: string | null
  downloadsCount: number
  starsAvg: string  // decimal average, e.g. "4.5"
  tagIds: string[]  // array of tag IDs
}
```

#### **List Endpoint** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/extensions.get.ts` (lines 1–25)

- **Input:** Query params (filters: `q`, `scope`, `category`, `dept`, `creator`, `publisher`, `tags`, `tagMatch`, `sort`, `page`, etc.)
- **Output:** `{ items: ExtensionListItem[], total: number, filters: ParsedFilters }`
- Flow:
  1. Parse search params into `Filters` type via `parseFilters(searchParamsToInput(...))`
  2. Call `extensionsRepo.findManyForList(db, filters)` (likely uses SQL + FTS)
  3. Call `extensionsRepo.countFiltered(db, filters)` in parallel
  4. Return both + echo back the parsed filters

#### **Facets Endpoint** — `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/facets.get.ts` (lines 1–29)

- **Input:** None (query-string-less; facets don't depend on filters per code comment)
- **Output:** `{ creators: CreatorFacet[], publishers: PublisherFacet[], tags: TagFacet[] }`
- Called once at page load, reused across filter navigations
- Facet types include `id`, `name` (+ `nameZh` for publishers), `count`

### 7. ACCENT USAGE PER DECISION #11

**Allowed accent moments on the list page:**
- ✅ Card hover state: `hover:border-(--color-accent)/30` (subtle tint on border)
- ✅ Tag drawer count badge: `bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg)` (active filter highlight)
- ✅ Tag drawer "Any/All" toggle buttons: `bg-(--color-accent)` when active (matching tag pill logic)
- ✅ Sort dropdown focus ring: `focus-visible:ring-(--color-accent)` (UI primitive focus ring — exempt)
- ✅ Install button (child of ExtCard): uses accent as primary CTA
- ✅ "View Panorama" link in header: `text-(--color-accent)` (primary link to editorial content)

**Not used on list page (per decision #11):**
- ❌ Category/Scope buttons: use bold weight + neutral border, not accent fills
- ❌ DeptPicker active state: `border-(--color-ink)/35`, not accent
- ❌ Sidebar nav active states: left rule or bold weight, no accent fills
- ❌ FilterChips active states: ink-based toggle, not accent

---

### 8. VISUAL HIERARCHY & TYPOGRAPHY

**Text scales:**
- `text-3xl` — Page title ("Browse Extensions")
- `text-[14px]` — Card name (bold)
- `text-[12.5px]` — Card description (muted, 2-line clamp)
- `text-[12px]` — Filter labels, filter buttons
- `text-[11px]` — Secondary controls (tag count badges, department depth indicators)
- `text-[10.5px]` — Tag pills
- `text-[10px]` — Badge labels

**Font weights:**
- `font-serif` — headings, sidebar section headers (italic for styling)
- `font-semibold` / `font-bold` — active states, important labels
- `font-mono` — counts, version numbers, code snippets

---

### 9. RESPONSIVE BEHAVIOR

**Mobile adjustments:**
- Sidebar: collapses to `w-0` (toggle via hamburger), transitions smoothly
- TopBar: hamburger button visible on all sizes; Explore dropdown hidden below `md:` breakpoint
- Filter bar: maintains row structure but may wrap on smaller viewports
- ExtGrid: auto-fill with `minmax(280px, 1fr)` — adapts from 1 column on small screens to multiple on desktop
- Search input: `max-w-xl` centered, scales with viewport

---

### 10. EMPTY STATES & LOADING

**No results:**
- Icon: SearchX from lucide-vue-next (muted color)
- Message: context-aware (no query results / filtered state / no extensions yet)
- "Suggest": link to adjust filters or check back later
- Rounded dashed border, light card background

**Loading:**
- NuxtLoadingIndicator at top: `color="var(--color-accent)"`, height 2px

---

### 11. CURRENT ACCENT MOMENTS ON LIST PAGE

Per CLAUDE.md decision #11 and tailwind.css, there are **exactly 3 intentional accent moments** on the list page:
1. **Card hover border tint** — subtle visual feedback
2. **Tag drawer active filter badges** — clear indication of selected filters
3. **"View Panorama" link** — editorial call-to-action to sister feature

The **Install button** on each card uses accent as a primary CTA (child component, outside scope of list page design).

## No Current Screenshots

The project README (`README.md`) and docs do not include design mockups or screenshots — the implementation is the source of truth. The design system is fully defined in the CSS custom properties + Tailwind v4 configuration + Vue component markup.

This mapping covers the **full surface, data flow, and visual design** of the extension list page. The page is intentionally minimal and elegant: a simple grid-based card layout with a multi-row filter bar above it, all anchored by a sticky top bar and a collapsible functional taxonomy sidebar. Decision #11 keeps accent usage sparse and intentional — only the primary CTA (Install button), filter highlights, and editorial links carry the accent color.

> AGENT

I've explored the list page. It's already well-built and follows decision #11 carefully (sparse accent usage). The main "elegance" opportunities I see:

- **Card density** — fragmented type scale (10/10.5/11/12/12.5/13/14px), two-row card header (name + dept tag on separate rows), and no cap on tag pills makes cards feel busy
- **Filter chrome weight** — three stacked rows ("Scope:" / "Sort:" labels, hard border-bottom) sit above content before you see a single card
- **No active-filter summary** — once you've applied 3 filters, there's no editorial "you're viewing X" line; the count alone doesn't convey what's filtered
- **No loading skeleton** — top progress bar only; cards pop in after fetch
- **Editorial header is plain** — only the serif title carries the Ivory editorial voice; the rest is utilitarian

Let me ask which direction you want before writing the plan.

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { ChevronDown } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import type { Locale, TagFacet } from "~~/shared/types"
4	import { tagLabel } from "~~/shared/tags"
5	
6	const props = defineProps<{
7	  tags: TagFacet[]
8	}>()
9	
10	const { t, locale } = useI18n()
11	const { filters, update } = useFilters()
12	
13	const TAG_COLLAPSE_LIMIT = 14
14	
15	const open = ref(false)
16	const showAll = ref(false)
17	
18	const activeTags = computed<string[]>(() => filters.value.tags ?? [])
19	const matchMode = computed(() => filters.value.tagMatch ?? "any")
20	
21	const visibleTags = computed(() => {
22	  if (showAll.value) return props.tags
23	  return props.tags.slice(0, TAG_COLLAPSE_LIMIT)
24	})
25	const hiddenCount = computed(() => Math.max(0, props.tags.length - TAG_COLLAPSE_LIMIT))
26	
27	function toggleTag(id: string) {
28	  const set = new Set(activeTags.value)
29	  if (set.has(id)) set.delete(id)
30	  else set.add(id)
31	  const next = Array.from(set)
32	  update({ tags: next.length ? next : undefined })
33	}
34	
35	function setMatch(mode: "any" | "all") {
36	  update({ tagMatch: mode === "any" ? undefined : mode })
37	}
38	
39	function clearTags() {
40	  update({ tags: undefined, tagMatch: undefined })
41	}
42	
43	function labelFor(id: string): string {
44	  return tagLabel(id, locale.value as Locale)
45	}
46	</script>
47	
48	<template>

50	    <button

60	      <span
61	        v-if="activeTags.length"
62	        class="rounded-full bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg) font-mono text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5"
63	      >
64	        {{ activeTags.length }}
65	      </span>
66	      <ChevronDown
67	        :size="12"
68	        :class="['transition-transform', open ? '' : '-rotate-90']"
69	        aria-hidden="true"
70	      />
71	    </button>
72	
73	    <div v-if="open" class="mt-3 rounded-md border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-3">

1	import {
2	  parseFilters,
3	  searchParamsToInput,
4	  serializeFilters,
5	  type Filters,
6	} from "~~/shared/validators/filters"
7	
8	export type FilterUpdate = Partial<Filters>
9	
10	export function useFilters() {
11	  const route = useRoute()
12	  const router = useRouter()
13	  const localePath = useLocalePath()
14	
15	  const filters = computed<Filters>(() => {
16	    const params = new URLSearchParams()
17	    for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(route.query)) {
18	      if (Array.isArray(v)) {
19	        for (const item of v) if (item !== null) params.append(k, String(item))
20	      } else if (v !== null && v !== undefined) {
21	        params.append(k, String(v))
22	      }
23	    }
24	    return parseFilters(searchParamsToInput(params))
25	  })
26	
27	  function update(partial: FilterUpdate) {
28	    const next: FilterUpdate = { ...filters.value, ...partial }
29	    if (!("page" in partial)) next.page = undefined
30	    const params = serializeFilters(next)
31	    const qs = params.toString()
32	    const path = route.path
33	    router.replace(qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path)
34	  }
35	
36	  function hrefForFilters(partial: FilterUpdate, basePath = "/extensions") {
37	    const next: FilterUpdate = { ...filters.value, ...partial }
38	    if (!("page" in partial)) next.page = undefined
39	    const params = serializeFilters(next)
40	    const qs = params.toString()
41	    const path = localePath(basePath)
42	    return qs ? `${path}?${qs}` : path
43	  }
44	
45	  return { filters, update, hrefForFilters }
46	}
47	
```

1	import { z } from "zod";
2	
3	const CATEGORIES = ["skills", "mcp", "slash", "plugins"] as const;
4	const SCOPES = ["personal", "org", "enterprise"] as const;
5	const FUNC_CATS = ["workTask", "business", "tools"] as const;
6	const FILTER_CHIPS = ["all", "trending", "new", "official", "free"] as const;
7	const SORTS = ["downloads", "stars", "recent"] as const;
8	const TAG_MATCHES = ["any", "all"] as const;
9	
10	export const PAGE_SIZE = 24;
11	
12	export const filtersSchema = z.object({
13	  q: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120).optional(),
14	  category: z.enum(CATEGORIES).optional(),
15	  scope: z.enum(SCOPES).optional(),
16	  funcCat: z.enum(FUNC_CATS).optional(),
17	  subCat: z.string().trim().min(1).max(40).optional(),
18	  l2: z.string().trim().min(1).max(40).optional(),
19	  // Department id (dotted-path) or the literal "__all" to disable the filter.
20	  dept: z.string().trim().min(1).max(120).optional(),
21	  // Creator = users.id (the user who published). Schema column is named
22	  // publisherUserId for legacy reasons; we expose it as "creator" everywhere.
23	  creator: z.string().trim().min(1).max(80).optional(),
24	  // Publisher = organizations.id (the owning org).
25	  publisher: z.string().trim().min(1).max(80).optional(),
26	  tags: z.array(z.string().trim().min(1).max(40)).max(16).optional(),
27	  tagMatch: z.enum(TAG_MATCHES).optional(),
28	  filter: z.enum(FILTER_CHIPS).optional(),
29	  sort: z.enum(SORTS).optional(),
30	  page: z.coerce.number().int().min(1).max(1000).optional(),
31	});
32	
33	export type Filters = z.infer<typeof filtersSchema>;
34	
35	/**
36	 * Parse Next.js searchParams into typed Filters. Invalid input is dropped,
37	 * so a malformed URL silently falls back to "no filter" rather than 500ing.
38	 */
39	export function parseFilters(
40	  input: Record<string, string | string[] | undefined>,
41	): Filters {
42	  const tags = input.tags;
43	  const normalized = {
44	    ...input,
45	    tags:
46	      tags === undefined
47	        ? undefined
48	        : Array.isArray(tags)
49	          ? tags
50	          : tags.split(",").filter(Boolean),
51	  };
52	  const parsed = filtersSchema.safeParse(normalized);
53	  return parsed.success ? parsed.data : {};
54	}
55	
56	export function pageOffset(page: number | undefined) {
57	  return ((page ?? 1) - 1) * PAGE_SIZE;
58	}
59	
60	/**
61	 * Adapter from `URLSearchParams` (the runtime shape) to the `Record` shape
62	 * `parseFilters` consumes. Repeated keys (`?tags=a&tags=b`) accumulate into
63	 * an array; `serializeFilters` always emits comma-joined, but external
64	 * links may use the array form and the validator already handles both.
65	 */
66	export function searchParamsToInput(
67	  searchParams: URLSearchParams,
68	): Record<string, string | string[]> {
69	  const out: Record<string, string | string[]> = {};
70	  for (const [key, value] of searchParams.entries()) {
71	    const prev = out[key];
72	    if (prev === undefined) {
73	      out[key] = value;
74	    } else if (Array.isArray(prev)) {
75	      prev.push(value);
76	    } else {
77	      out[key] = [prev, value];
78	    }
79	  }
80	  return out;
81	}
82	
83	/**
84	 * Inverse of `parseFilters`. Encodes typed filters back into URL search
85	 * params using the same conventions parseFilters consumes — comma-joined
86	 * arrays for `tags`, omitted keys for undefined / empty values.
87	 */
88	export function serializeFilters(filters: Partial<Filters>): URLSearchParams {
89	  const params = new URLSearchParams();
90	  for (const [key, value] of Object.entries(filters)) {
91	    if (value === undefined || value === "" || value === null) continue;
92	    if (Array.isArray(value)) {
93	      if (value.length > 0) params.set(key, value.join(","));
94	    } else {
95	      params.set(key, String(value));
96	    }
97	  }
98	  return params;
99	}
100

17	const triggerLabel = computed(() => {

24	// Path of dept IDs from the active dept up to (and including) the root.

28	  const parts = targetId.split(".")

30	    set.add(parts.slice(0, i).join("."))

46	  expanded.value = next

56	function flatten(dept: Department, depth: number): Array<{ dept: Department; depth: number }> {
57	  const out: Array<{ dept: Department; depth: number }> = [{ dept, depth }]

59	    for (const child of dept.children) {

61	    }
62	  }

64	}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/let-s-take-a-look-sequential-beaver.md",
  "content": "# Extension list page — elegance pass\n\n## Context\n\nThe list page (`/en/extensions`) is functionally complete but reads as utilitarian rather than editorial. Four issues surface on inspection:\n\n1. **ExtCard has fragmented typography** — seven micro font-sizes (10/10.5/11/12/12.5/13/14px) and a two-row header (name+badge on row 1, dept-pill on row 2) make cards feel busy. Tag pills aren't capped — cards with 6+ tags overwhelm the description.\n2. **No active-filter summary** — once filters are applied, the only feedback is the raw count. There's no editorial line communicating *what* is filtered, and no per-chip dismiss affordance. \"Clear filters\" only appears in the empty state.\n3. **FilterBar chrome is heavy** — three rows, two utilitarian labels (\"Scope:\", \"Sort:\"), and a hard border-bottom. Sort logically describes *output* (how results are ordered) but currently sits inside the *input* bar.\n4. **No skeleton on first paint** — the initial render shows nothing until `useFetch` resolves; subsequent navigations keep previous cards visible.\n\nGoal: tighten the page into something that reads as deliberately composed — without changing what's possible. Same filters, same data, more elegant surface. One PR, several commits.\n\n## Approach\n\nFour commits, each independently reviewable. Sort is relocated from FilterBar (input) to a new ResultsSummary band (output), where it sits next to the count and dismissible active-filter chips. ExtCard's type scale collapses from seven sizes to four. Skeleton lands last.\n\n### Commit 1 — `refactor(ext-list): tighten ExtCard hierarchy and typography`\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue`\n\nChanges:\n\n- **Type scale**: collapse to 4 sizes\n  - `text-[15px]` — card name (was `text-[14px]`, gains presence)\n  - `text-[13px]` — description (was `text-[12.5px]`)\n  - `text-[11px]` — meta (tags, dept, badge — was scattered 10/10.5/11)\n  - `text-[12px]` font-mono — stats (stars, downloads — unchanged)\n- **Header row**: collapse name+badge+dept onto a single line. Icon box + (name · badge) inline. Move dept out of the icon-aligned column.\n- **Dept placement**: render dept as plain text (Building2 icon + leaf label) on a dedicated line *below* the description, in `text-(--color-ink-muted) text-[11px]`. Drops the bordered pill. \"Mine\" emphasis becomes `text-(--color-accent)` instead of border+bg tint (still allowed by decision #11 since accent on text only is consistent with how accent links read).\n  - *Alternative kept in reserve*: if dept-as-text feels too quiet, restore a faint left-rule (`border-l border-(--color-ink)/15 pl-2`) instead of the pill.\n- **Tag cap**: limit visible tag pills to 3, append `+N` indicator (same styling, but `border-(--color-border)/60 text-(--color-ink-muted)/70`). Avoids 6-tag cards eating two lines.\n- **Hover refinement**: add `hover:shadow-[0_2px_8px_-4px_oklch(0_0_0_/_0.08)]` for a subtle crafted lift on top of the existing `-translate-y-0.5 + border-accent/30`. Skip in dark theme via CSS layer or accept the muted result (shadow on dark cards is barely visible anyway).\n- **Icon box**: bump to size-11 (was size-10) to balance the larger name. Keep the tinted background+border formula.\n- **Badge weight**: reduce from `font-bold` to `font-semibold` and lower contrast slightly so the name stays primary.\n\nNo prop changes; component contract is identical.\n\n### Commit 2 — `feat(ext-list): add ResultsSummary with active filter chips`\n\n**New file:** `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue`\n\nRenders a horizontal band that sits between FilterBar and ExtGrid. Layout:\n\n```\n[Showing 142 extensions]   [×Engineering] [×Trending] [×react]   [Clear all]   |   [Sort: Downloads ▾]\n└─── left ───┘             └─── center: dismissible chips ───┘                  └── right ──┘\n```\n\nProps:\n\n```ts\ndefineProps<{\n  count: number\n  creators: CreatorFacet[]\n  publishers: PublisherFacet[]\n  tags: TagFacet[]\n}>()\n```\n\nInternally uses `useFilters()` (`filters` + `update`) and `useI18n()`. Resolves display names by joining facet arrays:\n\n- `query` → just shows the quoted string, dismiss = `update({ q: undefined })`\n- `scope` → `t('filters.scope.${scope}')`\n- `dept` → `deptPath(id, locale).join(' / ')` from `shared/data/departments.ts`\n- `creator` → `creators.find(c => c.id === filters.creator)?.name`\n- `publisher` → locale-aware name from publisher facet\n- `filter` → `t('filters.chips.${filter}')` (Trending/New/etc.)\n- `tags` → one chip per tag id, label via `tagLabel(id, locale)` from `shared/tags.ts`\n- `tagMatch` → not directly removable (derived from tags presence; clears when last tag clears)\n- `category`, `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2` → out of scope for this band (they're set from sidebar nav, not filters UI); skip\n\nChip styling: small pill with × icon (lucide `X`), neutral border, hover darkens border + ink. No accent fill (per decision #11 — these are state indicators, not active CTAs).\n\n\"Clear all\" link: shown only when `activeChipsCount > 0`. Calls `update({ q: undefined, scope: undefined, dept: undefined, creator: undefined, publisher: undefined, filter: undefined, tags: undefined, tagMatch: undefined })`.\n\nWhen zero filters active, the band shows only the count on the left and Sort on the right — same height, so the page doesn't jump as filters change.\n\n**Wire-up in** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`:\n- Remove the `<p>` count line from the header — `ResultsSummary` owns it now\n- Remove the `filtersActive` computed (ResultsSummary derives this internally)\n- Insert `<ResultsSummary :count=\"total\" :creators=\"...\" :publishers=\"...\" :tags=\"...\" />` between `<FilterBar>` and `<ExtGrid>`\n- Header becomes: title alone on the left, Panorama link on the right (when MCP filter active)\n\n**i18n additions** (en + zh):\n\n```json\n\"extensions\": {\n  \"summary\": {\n    \"showing\": \"Showing {count}\",\n    \"showingZero\": \"No matches\",\n    \"clearAll\": \"Clear all\",\n    \"removeFilter\": \"Remove {label} filter\"\n  }\n}\n```\n\n(`zh` mirrors: `\"showing\": \"显示 {count} 个\"`, `\"clearAll\": \"清除全部\"`, etc.)\n\n### Commit 3 — `refactor(ext-list): move SortSelect to ResultsSummary, soften FilterBar`\n\n**Files:**\n- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — remove third row's `<SortSelect />`; keep only `<FilterChips />` on row 3 (now a single-control row, becomes `justify-start`). Soften the bottom border: `border-b border-(--color-border)/60 pb-3` (was `pb-4`).\n- `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — remove the `<span>Scope:</span>` label; rely on the four button names being self-evident.\n- `app/components/filters/SortSelect.vue` — drop the `<span>Sort:</span>` label, replace with a lucide `ArrowDownUp` icon prefix; keep the `<select>` underneath. Result is a compact `[⇅ Downloads ▾]` control suitable for the right edge of ResultsSummary.\n- `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — render `<SortSelect />` in its right slot.\n\nNo behavioral change — Sort still writes to `filters.sort`.\n\n### Commit 4 — `feat(ext-list): add ExtGridSkeleton for first paint`\n\n**New file:** `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue`\n\nRenders 8 placeholder cards using the same `grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))] gap-4` shell. Each placeholder mimics ExtCard's silhouette:\n\n- Icon box (size-11, neutral bg-(--color-border)/40)\n- Two stacked lines for name + description (rounded bars, `bg-(--color-border)/40`)\n- Three tag pill silhouettes\n- Footer line with two short bars + button-sized bar on the right\n\nUses Tailwind's `animate-pulse` for the subtle breathing effect.\n\n**Wire-up in** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`:\n- Destructure `pending` from `useFetch`: `const { data, pending, refresh } = await useFetch(...)`\n- Render `<ExtGridSkeleton v-if=\"pending && items.length === 0\" />` and `<ExtGrid v-else ... />`\n\nThis only shows on first paint where SSR is not in play (e.g., client-side navigation from an SPA route, or hard refresh with hydration mismatch). With SSR enabled it's effectively a defense-in-depth — useful when fetch fails or is slow but data not yet hydrated.\n\n## Critical files\n\n**Edited:**\n- `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue` — commit 1\n- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — commits 2, 4\n- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — commit 3\n- `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — commit 3\n- `app/components/filters/SortSelect.vue` — commit 3\n- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — commit 2\n\n**Created:**\n- `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — commit 2\n- `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue` — commit 4\n\n## Reused existing utilities\n\n- `useFilters()` (`app/composables/useFilters.ts:10`) — `filters` reactive ref + `update(partial)` mutator. ResultsSummary chip dismissal calls `update({ key: undefined })`.\n- `deptPath(id, locale)` and `findDept(id)` (`shared/data/departments.ts`) — for dept chip label.\n- `tagLabel(id, locale)` (`shared/tags.ts`) — tag chip labels.\n- `CreatorFacet`, `PublisherFacet`, `TagFacet` types (`shared/types.ts`) — ResultsSummary props.\n- Existing badge classes (`.badge-official`, `.badge-popular`, `.badge-new`) — unchanged.\n- Existing CSS vars (`--color-ink`, `--color-ink-muted`, `--color-border`, `--color-accent`, `--color-card`, `--radius-card`) — all three themes already define them.\n\n## Accent budget (decision #11)\n\nThe list page already lives within the budget. After this pass:\n\n- ✅ Card hover border tint — unchanged\n- ✅ TagDrawer active filter badge — unchanged\n- ✅ \"View Panorama\" link — unchanged\n- ✅ Install button on cards — unchanged\n- ✅ Dept \"Mine\" emphasis: now text-only accent (was border+bg) — strictly lighter accent footprint\n- ❌ ResultsSummary chips: deliberately neutral, not tinted\n\nNet result: equal or slightly *less* accent tint than today.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. **Local dev**: `bun dev` (or `pnpm dev`) → open `http://localhost:3000/en/extensions`.\n2. **Empty state**: search for `xyzxyz` — verify ExtGrid empty card still appears; ResultsSummary should hide \"Clear all\" iff `filtersActive` is false (the search itself is a filter, so should show with a `×\"xyzxyz\"` chip).\n3. **All filter chip types**: apply scope=org, dept=eng.cloud, creator=<first creator>, publisher=<first org>, filter=trending, tags=[a,b], q=\"claude\" — verify each renders as a dismissible chip with the correct localized label, and dismissing each one removes only that filter.\n4. **Clear all**: with multiple filters set, click \"Clear all\" — URL should drop to `/en/extensions` (no query string).\n5. **Sort relocation**: change Sort to Stars from the ResultsSummary band — verify URL gains `?sort=stars` and the grid re-orders.\n6. **Locale parity**: switch to `/zh/extensions` — chips and \"Clear all\" render in Chinese; dept name uses `nameZh`.\n7. **Theme parity**: cycle Ivory → Dark → Mono Clean. Card hover shadow may not appear in Dark (expected); skeleton placeholders should be visible in all three.\n8. **Tag cap**: find an extension with ≥4 tags (or seed one) — confirm only 3 pills render with `+N` indicator.\n9. **Mobile**: collapse viewport to 375px — ResultsSummary chips should wrap; FilterBar pickers should already wrap (existing behavior preserved).\n10. **Type-check + lint**: `bun run typecheck && bun run lint` per `docs/plan.md` validate pipeline.\n11. **Coverage**: no logic changes — existing tests should pass without modification. ResultsSummary is presentational; chip-dismiss behavior is exercised through `useFilters` which is already covered.\n\n## Out of scope\n\n- Pagination polish, infinite scroll\n- Density toggle (list vs. grid view)\n- Card SaveButton / favorites surfacing on hover\n- Filter persistence across sessions\n- Sidebar refinement\n- Detail page changes\n"
}

## Approved Plan:
# Extension list page — elegance pass

## Context

The list page (`/en/extensions`) is functionally complete but reads as utilitarian rather than editorial. Four issues surface on inspection:

1. **ExtCard has fragmented typography** — seven micro font-sizes (10/10.5/11/12/12.5/13/14px) and a two-row header (name+badge on row 1, dept-pill on row 2) make cards feel busy. Tag pills aren't capped — cards with 6+ tags overwhelm the description.
2. **No active-filter summary** — once filters are applied, the only feedback is the raw count. There's no editorial line communicating *what* is filtered, and no per-chip dismiss affordance. "Clear filters" only appears in the empty state.
3. **FilterBar chrome is heavy** — three rows, two utilitarian labels ("Scope:", "Sort:"), and a hard border-bottom. Sort logically describes *output* (how results are ordered) but currently sits inside the *input* bar.
4. **No skeleton on first paint** — the initial render shows nothing until `useFetch` resolves; subsequent navigations keep previous cards visible.

Goal: tighten the page into something that reads as deliberately composed — without changing what's possible. Same filters, same data, more elegant surface. One PR, several commits.

## Approach

Four commits, each independently reviewable. Sort is relocated from FilterBar (input) to a new ResultsSummary band (output), where it sits next to the count and dismissible active-filter chips. ExtCard's type scale collapses from seven sizes to four. Skeleton lands last.

### Commit 1 — `refactor(ext-list): tighten ExtCard hierarchy and typography`

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue`

Changes:

- **Type scale**: collapse to 4 sizes
  - `text-[15px]` — card name (was `text-[14px]`, gains presence)
  - `text-[13px]` — description (was `text-[12.5px]`)
  - `text-[11px]` — meta (tags, dept, badge — was scattered 10/10.5/11)
  - `text-[12px]` font-mono — stats (stars, downloads — unchanged)
- **Header row**: collapse name+badge+dept onto a single line. Icon box + (name · badge) inline. Move dept out of the icon-aligned column.
- **Dept placement**: render dept as plain text (Building2 icon + leaf label) on a dedicated line *below* the description, in `text-(--color-ink-muted) text-[11px]`. Drops the bordered pill. "Mine" emphasis becomes `text-(--color-accent)` instead of border+bg tint (still allowed by decision #11 since accent on text only is consistent with how accent links read).
  - *Alternative kept in reserve*: if dept-as-text feels too quiet, restore a faint left-rule (`border-l border-(--color-ink)/15 pl-2`) instead of the pill.
- **Tag cap**: limit visible tag pills to 3, append `+N` indicator (same styling, but `border-(--color-border)/60 text-(--color-ink-muted)/70`). Avoids 6-tag cards eating two lines.
- **Hover refinement**: add `hover:shadow-[0_2px_8px_-4px_oklch(0_0_0_/_0.08)]` for a subtle crafted lift on top of the existing `-translate-y-0.5 + border-accent/30`. Skip in dark theme via CSS layer or accept the muted result (shadow on dark cards is barely visible anyway).
- **Icon box**: bump to size-11 (was size-10) to balance the larger name. Keep the tinted background+border formula.
- **Badge weight**: reduce from `font-bold` to `font-semibold` and lower contrast slightly so the name stays primary.

No prop changes; component contract is identical.

### Commit 2 — `feat(ext-list): add ResultsSummary with active filter chips`

**New file:** `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue`

Renders a horizontal band that sits between FilterBar and ExtGrid. Layout:

```
[Showing 142 extensions]   [×Engineering] [×Trending] [×react]   [Clear all]   |   [Sort: Downloads ▾]
└─── left ───┘             └─── center: dismissible chips ───┘                  └── right ──┘
```

Props:

```ts
defineProps<{
  count: number
  creators: CreatorFacet[]
  publishers: PublisherFacet[]
  tags: TagFacet[]
}>()
```

Internally uses `useFilters()` (`filters` + `update`) and `useI18n()`. Resolves display names by joining facet arrays:

- `query` → just shows the quoted string, dismiss = `update({ q: undefined })`
- `scope` → `t('filters.scope.${scope}')`
- `dept` → `deptPath(id, locale).join(' / ')` from `shared/data/departments.ts`
- `creator` → `creators.find(c => c.id === filters.creator)?.name`
- `publisher` → locale-aware name from publisher facet
- `filter` → `t('filters.chips.${filter}')` (Trending/New/etc.)
- `tags` → one chip per tag id, label via `tagLabel(id, locale)` from `shared/tags.ts`
- `tagMatch` → not directly removable (derived from tags presence; clears when last tag clears)
- `category`, `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2` → out of scope for this band (they're set from sidebar nav, not filters UI); skip

Chip styling: small pill with × icon (lucide `X`), neutral border, hover darkens border + ink. No accent fill (per decision #11 — these are state indicators, not active CTAs).

"Clear all" link: shown only when `activeChipsCount > 0`. Calls `update({ q: undefined, scope: undefined, dept: undefined, creator: undefined, publisher: undefined, filter: undefined, tags: undefined, tagMatch: undefined })`.

When zero filters active, the band shows only the count on the left and Sort on the right — same height, so the page doesn't jump as filters change.

**Wire-up in** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`:
- Remove the `<p>` count line from the header — `ResultsSummary` owns it now
- Remove the `filtersActive` computed (ResultsSummary derives this internally)
- Insert `<ResultsSummary :count="total" :creators="..." :publishers="..." :tags="..." />` between `<FilterBar>` and `<ExtGrid>`
- Header becomes: title alone on the left, Panorama link on the right (when MCP filter active)

**i18n additions** (en + zh):

```json
"extensions": {
  "summary": {
    "showing": "Showing {count}",
    "showingZero": "No matches",
    "clearAll": "Clear all",
    "removeFilter": "Remove {label} filter"
  }
}
```

(`zh` mirrors: `"showing": "显示 {count} 个"`, `"clearAll": "清除全部"`, etc.)

### Commit 3 — `refactor(ext-list): move SortSelect to ResultsSummary, soften FilterBar`

**Files:**
- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — remove third row's `<SortSelect />`; keep only `<FilterChips />` on row 3 (now a single-control row, becomes `justify-start`). Soften the bottom border: `border-b border-(--color-border)/60 pb-3` (was `pb-4`).
- `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — remove the `<span>Scope:</span>` label; rely on the four button names being self-evident.
- `app/components/filters/SortSelect.vue` — drop the `<span>Sort:</span>` label, replace with a lucide `ArrowDownUp` icon prefix; keep the `<select>` underneath. Result is a compact `[⇅ Downloads ▾]` control suitable for the right edge of ResultsSummary.
- `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — render `<SortSelect />` in its right slot.

No behavioral change — Sort still writes to `filters.sort`.

### Commit 4 — `feat(ext-list): add ExtGridSkeleton for first paint`

**New file:** `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue`

Renders 8 placeholder cards using the same `grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))] gap-4` shell. Each placeholder mimics ExtCard's silhouette:

- Icon box (size-11, neutral bg-(--color-border)/40)
- Two stacked lines for name + description (rounded bars, `bg-(--color-border)/40`)
- Three tag pill silhouettes
- Footer line with two short bars + button-sized bar on the right

Uses Tailwind's `animate-pulse` for the subtle breathing effect.

**Wire-up in** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`:
- Destructure `pending` from `useFetch`: `const { data, pending, refresh } = await useFetch(...)`
- Render `<ExtGridSkeleton v-if="pending && items.length === 0" />` and `<ExtGrid v-else ... />`

This only shows on first paint where SSR is not in play (e.g., client-side navigation from an SPA route, or hard refresh with hydration mismatch). With SSR enabled it's effectively a defense-in-depth — useful when fetch fails or is slow but data not yet hydrated.

## Critical files

**Edited:**
- `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue` — commit 1
- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — commits 2, 4
- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — commit 3
- `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — commit 3
- `app/components/filters/SortSelect.vue` — commit 3
- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — commit 2

**Created:**
- `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — commit 2
- `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue` — commit 4

## Reused existing utilities

- `useFilters()` (`app/composables/useFilters.ts:10`) — `filters` reactive ref + `update(partial)` mutator. ResultsSummary chip dismissal calls `update({ key: undefined })`.
- `deptPath(id, locale)` and `findDept(id)` (`shared/data/departments.ts`) — for dept chip label.
- `tagLabel(id, locale)` (`shared/tags.ts`) — tag chip labels.
- `CreatorFacet`, `PublisherFacet`, `TagFacet` types (`shared/types.ts`) — ResultsSummary props.
- Existing badge classes (`.badge-official`, `.badge-popular`, `.badge-new`) — unchanged.
- Existing CSS vars (`--color-ink`, `--color-ink-muted`, `--color-border`, `--color-accent`, `--color-card`, `--radius-card`) — all three themes already define them.

## Accent budget (decision #11)

The list page already lives within the budget. After this pass:

- ✅ Card hover border tint — unchanged
- ✅ TagDrawer active filter badge — unchanged
- ✅ "View Panorama" link — unchanged
- ✅ Install button on cards — unchanged
- ✅ Dept "Mine" emphasis: now text-only accent (was border+bg) — strictly lighter accent footprint
- ❌ ResultsSummary chips: deliberately neutral, not tinted

Net result: equal or slightly *less* accent tint than today.

## Verification

1. **Local dev**: `bun dev` (or `pnpm dev`) → open `http://localhost:3000/en/extensions`.
2. **Empty state**: search for `xyzxyz` — verify ExtGrid empty card still appears; ResultsSummary should hide "Clear all" iff `filtersActive` is false (the search itself is a filter, so should show with a `×"xyzxyz"` chip).
3. **All filter chip types**: apply scope=org, dept=eng.cloud, creator=<first creator>, publisher=<first org>, filter=trending, tags=[a,b], q="claude" — verify each renders as a dismissible chip with the correct localized label, and dismissing each one removes only that filter.
4. **Clear all**: with multiple filters set, click "Clear all" — URL should drop to `/en/extensions` (no query string).
5. **Sort relocation**: change Sort to Stars from the ResultsSummary band — verify URL gains `?sort=stars` and the grid re-orders.
6. **Locale parity**: switch to `/zh/extensions` — chips and "Clear all" render in Chinese; dept name uses `nameZh`.
7. **Theme parity**: cycle Ivory → Dark → Mono Clean. Card hover shadow may not appear in Dark (expected); skeleton placeholders should be visible in all three.
8. **Tag cap**: find an extension with ≥4 tags (or seed one) — confirm only 3 pills render with `+N` indicator.
9. **Mobile**: collapse viewport to 375px — ResultsSummary chips should wrap; FilterBar pickers should already wrap (existing behavior preserved).
10. **Type-check + lint**: `bun run typecheck && bun run lint` per `docs/plan.md` validate pipeline.
11. **Coverage**: no logic changes — existing tests should pass without modification. ResultsSummary is presentational; chip-dismiss behavior is exercised through `useFilters` which is already covered.

## Out of scope

- Pagination polish, infinite scroll
- Density toggle (list vs. grid view)
- Card SaveButton / favorites surfacing on hover
- Filter persistence across sessions
- Sidebar refinement
- Detail page changes

```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { X } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\nimport { deptPath } from \"~~/shared/data/departments\"\nimport { tagLabel } from \"~~/shared/tags\"\nimport type {\n  CreatorFacet,\n  Locale,\n  PublisherFacet,\n  TagFacet,\n} from \"~~/shared/types\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  count: number\n  creators: CreatorFacet[]\n  publishers: PublisherFacet[]\n  tags: TagFacet[]\n}>()\n\nconst { t, locale } = useI18n()\nconst { filters, update } = useFilters()\n\ninterface Chip {\n  key: string\n  label: string\n  removeLabel: string\n  onDismiss: () => void\n}\n\nfunction creatorName(id: string): string {\n  const c = props.creators.find((x) => x.id === id)\n  if (!c) return id\n  return c.name ?? c.email\n}\n\nfunction publisherName(id: string): string {\n  const p = props.publishers.find((x) => x.id === id)\n  if (!p) return id\n  return locale.value === \"zh\" && p.nameZh ? p.nameZh : p.name\n}\n\nconst chips = computed<Chip[]>(() => {\n  const f = filters.value\n  const out: Chip[] = []\n\n  if (f.q) {\n    const label = `\"${f.q}\"`\n    out.push({\n      key: \"q\",\n      label,\n      removeLabel: label,\n      onDismiss: () => update({ q: undefined }),\n    })\n  }\n  if (f.scope) {\n    const label = t(`filters.scope.${f.scope}`)\n    out.push({\n      key: \"scope\",\n      label,\n      removeLabel: label,\n      onDismiss: () => update({ scope: undefined }),\n    })\n  }\n  if (f.dept) {\n    const trail = deptPath(f.dept, locale.value as Locale)\n    const label = trail.length > 0 ? trail.join(\" / \") : f.dept\n    out.push({\n      key: \"dept\",\n      label,\n      removeLabel: label,\n      onDismiss: () => update({ dept: undefined }),\n    })\n  }\n  if (f.creator) {\n    const label = creatorName(f.creator)\n    out.push({\n      key: \"creator\",\n      label,\n      removeLabel: label,\n      onDismiss: () => update({ creator: undefined }),\n    })\n  }\n  if (f.publisher) {\n    const label = publisherName(f.publisher)\n    out.push({\n      key: \"publisher\",\n      label,\n      removeLabel: label,\n      onDismiss: () => update({ publisher: undefined }),\n    })\n  }\n  if (f.filter) {\n    const label = t(`filters.chips.${f.filter}`)\n    out.push({\n      key: \"filter\",\n      label,\n      removeLabel: label,\n      onDismiss: () => update({ filter: undefined }),\n    })\n  }\n  if (f.tags && f.tags.length > 0) {\n    for (const id of f.tags) {\n      const label = `#${tagLabel(id, locale.value as Locale)}`\n      out.push({\n        key: `tag:${id}`,\n        label,\n        removeLabel: label,\n        onDismiss: () => {\n          const next = (filters.value.tags ?? []).filter((t) => t !== id)\n          update({ tags: next.length ? next : undefined, tagMatch: next.length ? filters.value.tagMatch : undefined })\n        },\n      })\n    }\n  }\n\n  return out\n})\n\nconst hasChips = computed(() => chips.value.length > 0)\n\nfunction clearAll() {\n  update({\n    q: undefined,\n    scope: undefined,\n    dept: undefined,\n    creator: undefined,\n    publisher: undefined,\n    filter: undefined,\n    tags: undefined,\n    tagMatch: undefined,\n  })\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"mb-4 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-x-3 gap-y-2\">\n    <p class=\"text-(--color-ink-muted) font-mono text-[12px]\">\n      {{ count > 0 ? t(\"extensions.summary.showing\", { count }) : t(\"extensions.summary.showingZero\") }}\n    </p>\n\n    <div v-if=\"hasChips\" class=\"flex flex-1 flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5\">\n      <button\n        v-for=\"chip in chips\"\n        :key=\"chip.key\"\n        type=\"button\"\n        :aria-label=\"t('extensions.summary.removeFilter', { label: chip.removeLabel })\"\n        class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] font-semibold text-(--color-ink-muted) transition-colors hover:border-(--color-ink)/40 hover:text-(--color-ink)\"\n        @click=\"chip.onDismiss()\"\n      >\n        <span class=\"max-w-[14ch] truncate\">{{ chip.label }}</span>\n        <X :size=\"11\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n      </button>\n\n      <button\n        type=\"button\"\n        class=\"ml-1 text-[11px] font-semibold text-(--color-ink-muted) underline-offset-2 transition-colors hover:text-(--color-ink) hover:underline\"\n        @click=\"clearAll()\"\n      >\n        {{ t(\"extensions.summary.clearAll\") }}\n      </button>\n    </div>\n\n    <div :class=\"['ml-auto', hasChips ? '' : 'ml-auto']\">\n      <SortSelect />\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/extensions/index.vue",
  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ArrowUpRight } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\n\nconst { t } = useI18n()\nconst route = useRoute()\nconst { filters } = useFilters()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\nconst { data, refresh } = await useFetch(\"/api/internal/extensions\", {\n  query: computed(() => route.query),\n  default: () => ({ items: [], total: 0, filters: {} }),\n})\n\n// Facets don't depend on filters — fetch once and reuse across navigations.\nconst { data: facets } = await useFetch(\"/api/internal/facets\", {\n  default: () => ({ creators: [], publishers: [], tags: [] }),\n})\n\nwatch(() => route.fullPath, () => refresh())\n\nconst items = computed(() => data.value?.items ?? [])\nconst total = computed(() => data.value?.total ?? 0)\nconst query = computed(() => filters.value.q)\nconst filtersActive = computed(() => {\n  const f = filters.value\n  return Object.keys(f).some((k) => k !== \"page\" && f[k as keyof typeof f] !== undefined)\n})\nconst isMcpCategory = computed(() => route.query.category === \"mcp\")\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"px-6 py-8 max-w-7xl mx-auto\">\n    <header class=\"mb-6 flex items-end justify-between gap-4 flex-wrap\">\n      <h1 class=\"font-serif text-3xl tracking-tight text-(--color-ink)\">\n        {{ t(\"extensions.browseTitle\") }}\n      </h1>\n      <NuxtLink\n        v-if=\"isMcpCategory\"\n        :to=\"localePath('/mcp-panorama')\"\n        class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[13px] font-medium text-(--color-accent) hover:gap-2 transition-[gap]\"\n      >\n        {{ t(\"extensions.mcpPanoramaLink\") }}\n        <ArrowUpRight :size=\"14\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n      </NuxtLink>\n    </header>\n\n    <FilterBar\n      :creators=\"facets.creators\"\n      :publishers=\"facets.publishers\"\n      :tags=\"facets.tags\"\n    />\n\n    <ResultsSummary\n      :count=\"total\"\n      :creators=\"facets.creators\"\n      :publishers=\"facets.publishers\"\n      :tags=\"facets.tags\"\n    />\n\n    <ExtGrid\n      :items=\"items\"\n      :query=\"query\"\n      :clear-filters-href=\"filtersActive ? localePath('/extensions') : undefined\"\n    />\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

```
1	# Extension list page — elegance pass
2	
3	## Context
4	
5	The list page (`/en/extensions`) is functionally complete but reads as utilitarian rather than editorial. Four issues surface on inspection:
6	
7	1. **ExtCard has fragmented typography** — seven micro font-sizes (10/10.5/11/12/12.5/13/14px) and a two-row header (name+badge on row 1, dept-pill on row 2) make cards feel busy. Tag pills aren't capped — cards with 6+ tags overwhelm the description.
8	2. **No active-filter summary** — once filters are applied, the only feedback is the raw count. There's no editorial line communicating *what* is filtered, and no per-chip dismiss affordance. "Clear filters" only appears in the empty state.
9	3. **FilterBar chrome is heavy** — three rows, two utilitarian labels ("Scope:", "Sort:"), and a hard border-bottom. Sort logically describes *output* (how results are ordered) but currently sits inside the *input* bar.
10	4. **No skeleton on first paint** — the initial render shows nothing until `useFetch` resolves; subsequent navigations keep previous cards visible.
11	
12	Goal: tighten the page into something that reads as deliberately composed — without changing what's possible. Same filters, same data, more elegant surface. One PR, several commits.
13	
14	## Approach
15	
16	Four commits, each independently reviewable. Sort is relocated from FilterBar (input) to a new ResultsSummary band (output), where it sits next to the count and dismissible active-filter chips. ExtCard's type scale collapses from seven sizes to four. Skeleton lands last.
17	
18	### Commit 1 — `refactor(ext-list): tighten ExtCard hierarchy and typography`
19	
20	**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue`
21	
22	Changes:
23	
24	- **Type scale**: collapse to 4 sizes
25	  - `text-[15px]` — card name (was `text-[14px]`, gains presence)
26	  - `text-[13px]` — description (was `text-[12.5px]`)
27	  - `text-[11px]` — meta (tags, dept, badge — was scattered 10/10.5/11)
28	  - `text-[12px]` font-mono — stats (stars, downloads — unchanged)
29	- **Header row**: collapse name+badge+dept onto a single line. Icon box + (name · badge) inline. Move dept out of the icon-aligned column.
30	- **Dept placement**: render dept as plain text (Building2 icon + leaf label) on a dedicated line *below* the description, in `text-(--color-ink-muted) text-[11px]`. Drops the bordered pill. "Mine" emphasis becomes `text-(--color-accent)` instead of border+bg tint (still allowed by decision #11 since accent on text only is consistent with how accent links read).
31	  - *Alternative kept in reserve*: if dept-as-text feels too quiet, restore a faint left-rule (`border-l border-(--color-ink)/15 pl-2`) instead of the pill.
32	- **Tag cap**: limit visible tag pills to 3, append `+N` indicator (same styling, but `border-(--color-border)/60 text-(--color-ink-muted)/70`). Avoids 6-tag cards eating two lines.
33	- **Hover refinement**: add `hover:shadow-[0_2px_8px_-4px_oklch(0_0_0_/_0.08)]` for a subtle crafted lift on top of the existing `-translate-y-0.5 + border-accent/30`. Skip in dark theme via CSS layer or accept the muted result (shadow on dark cards is barely visible anyway).
34	- **Icon box**: bump to size-11 (was size-10) to balance the larger name. Keep the tinted background+border formula.
35	- **Badge weight**: reduce from `font-bold` to `font-semibold` and lower contrast slightly so the name stays primary.
36	
37	No prop changes; component contract is identical.
38	
39	### Commit 2 — `feat(ext-list): add ResultsSummary with active filter chips`
40	
41	**New file:** `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue`
42	
43	Renders a horizontal band that sits between FilterBar and ExtGrid. Layout:
44	
45	```
46	[Showing 142 extensions]   [×Engineering] [×Trending] [×react]   [Clear all]   |   [Sort: Downloads ▾]
47	└─── left ───┘             └─── center: dismissible chips ───┘                  └── right ──┘
48	```
49	
50	Props:
51	
52	```ts
53	defineProps<{
54	  count: number
55	  creators: CreatorFacet[]
56	  publishers: PublisherFacet[]
57	  tags: TagFacet[]
58	}>()
59	```
60	
61	Internally uses `useFilters()` (`filters` + `update`) and `useI18n()`. Resolves display names by joining facet arrays:
62	
63	- `query` → just shows the quoted string, dismiss = `update({ q: undefined })`
64	- `scope` → `t('filters.scope.${scope}')`
65	- `dept` → `deptPath(id, locale).join(' / ')` from `shared/data/departments.ts`
66	- `creator` → `creators.find(c => c.id === filters.creator)?.name`
67	- `publisher` → locale-aware name from publisher facet
68	- `filter` → `t('filters.chips.${filter}')` (Trending/New/etc.)
69	- `tags` → one chip per tag id, label via `tagLabel(id, locale)` from `shared/tags.ts`
70	- `tagMatch` → not directly removable (derived from tags presence; clears when last tag clears)
71	- `category`, `funcCat`, `subCat`, `l2` → out of scope for this band (they're set from sidebar nav, not filters UI); skip
72	
73	Chip styling: small pill with × icon (lucide `X`), neutral border, hover darkens border + ink. No accent fill (per decision #11 — these are state indicators, not active CTAs).
74	
75	"Clear all" link: shown only when `activeChipsCount > 0`. Calls `update({ q: undefined, scope: undefined, dept: undefined, creator: undefined, publisher: undefined, filter: undefined, tags: undefined, tagMatch: undefined })`.
76	
77	When zero filters active, the band shows only the count on the left and Sort on the right — same height, so the page doesn't jump as filters change.
78	
79	**Wire-up in** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`:
80	- Remove the `<p>` count line from the header — `ResultsSummary` owns it now
81	- Remove the `filtersActive` computed (ResultsSummary derives this internally)
82	- Insert `<ResultsSummary :count="total" :creators="..." :publishers="..." :tags="..." />` between `<FilterBar>` and `<ExtGrid>`
83	- Header becomes: title alone on the left, Panorama link on the right (when MCP filter active)
84	
85	**i18n additions** (en + zh):
86	
87	```json
88	"extensions": {
89	  "summary": {
90	    "showing": "Showing {count}",
91	    "showingZero": "No matches",
92	    "clearAll": "Clear all",
93	    "removeFilter": "Remove {label} filter"
94	  }
95	}
96	```
97	
98	(`zh` mirrors: `"showing": "显示 {count} 个"`, `"clearAll": "清除全部"`, etc.)
99	
100	### Commit 3 — `refactor(ext-list): move SortSelect to ResultsSummary, soften FilterBar`
101	
102	**Files:**
103	- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — remove third row's `<SortSelect />`; keep only `<FilterChips />` on row 3 (now a single-control row, becomes `justify-start`). Soften the bottom border: `border-b border-(--color-border)/60 pb-3` (was `pb-4`).
104	- `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — remove the `<span>Scope:</span>` label; rely on the four button names being self-evident.
105	- `app/components/filters/SortSelect.vue` — drop the `<span>Sort:</span>` label, replace with a lucide `ArrowDownUp` icon prefix; keep the `<select>` underneath. Result is a compact `[⇅ Downloads ▾]` control suitable for the right edge of ResultsSummary.
106	- `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — render `<SortSelect />` in its right slot.
107	
108	No behavioral change — Sort still writes to `filters.sort`.
109	
110	### Commit 4 — `feat(ext-list): add ExtGridSkeleton for first paint`
111	
112	**New file:** `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue`
113	
114	Renders 8 placeholder cards using the same `grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))] gap-4` shell. Each placeholder mimics ExtCard's silhouette:
115	
116	- Icon box (size-11, neutral bg-(--color-border)/40)
117	- Two stacked lines for name + description (rounded bars, `bg-(--color-border)/40`)
118	- Three tag pill silhouettes
119	- Footer line with two short bars + button-sized bar on the right
120	
121	Uses Tailwind's `animate-pulse` for the subtle breathing effect.
122	
123	**Wire-up in** `app/pages/extensions/index.vue`:
124	- Destructure `pending` from `useFetch`: `const { data, pending, refresh } = await useFetch(...)`
125	- Render `<ExtGridSkeleton v-if="pending && items.length === 0" />` and `<ExtGrid v-else ... />`
126	
127	This only shows on first paint where SSR is not in play (e.g., client-side navigation from an SPA route, or hard refresh with hydration mismatch). With SSR enabled it's effectively a defense-in-depth — useful when fetch fails or is slow but data not yet hydrated.
128	
129	## Critical files
130	
131	**Edited:**
132	- `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue` — commit 1
133	- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — commits 2, 4
134	- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — commit 3
135	- `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — commit 3
136	- `app/components/filters/SortSelect.vue` — commit 3
137	- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — commit 2
138	
139	**Created:**
140	- `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — commit 2
141	- `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue` — commit 4
142	
143	## Reused existing utilities
144	
145	- `useFilters()` (`app/composables/useFilters.ts:10`) — `filters` reactive ref + `update(partial)` mutator. ResultsSummary chip dismissal calls `update({ key: undefined })`.
146	- `deptPath(id, locale)` and `findDept(id)` (`shared/data/departments.ts`) — for dept chip label.
147	- `tagLabel(id, locale)` (`shared/tags.ts`) — tag chip labels.
148	- `CreatorFacet`, `PublisherFacet`, `TagFacet` types (`shared/types.ts`) — ResultsSummary props.
149	- Existing badge classes (`.badge-official`, `.badge-popular`, `.badge-new`) — unchanged.
150	- Existing CSS vars (`--color-ink`, `--color-ink-muted`, `--color-border`, `--color-accent`, `--color-card`, `--radius-card`) — all three themes already define them.
151	
152	## Accent budget (decision #11)
153	
154	The list page already lives within the budget. After this pass:
155	
156	- ✅ Card hover border tint — unchanged
157	- ✅ TagDrawer active filter badge — unchanged
158	- ✅ "View Panorama" link — unchanged
159	- ✅ Install button on cards — unchanged
160	- ✅ Dept "Mine" emphasis: now text-only accent (was border+bg) — strictly lighter accent footprint
161	- ❌ ResultsSummary chips: deliberately neutral, not tinted
162	
163	Net result: equal or slightly *less* accent tint than today.
164	
165	## Verification
166	
167	1. **Local dev**: `bun dev` (or `pnpm dev`) → open `http://localhost:3000/en/extensions`.
168	2. **Empty state**: search for `xyzxyz` — verify ExtGrid empty card still appears; ResultsSummary should hide "Clear all" iff `filtersActive` is false (the search itself is a filter, so should show with a `×"xyzxyz"` chip).
169	3. **All filter chip types**: apply scope=org, dept=eng.cloud, creator=<first creator>, publisher=<first org>, filter=trending, tags=[a,b], q="claude" — verify each renders as a dismissible chip with the correct localized label, and dismissing each one removes only that filter.
170	4. **Clear all**: with multiple filters set, click "Clear all" — URL should drop to `/en/extensions` (no query string).
171	5. **Sort relocation**: change Sort to Stars from the ResultsSummary band — verify URL gains `?sort=stars` and the grid re-orders.
172	6. **Locale parity**: switch to `/zh/extensions` — chips and "Clear all" render in Chinese; dept name uses `nameZh`.
173	7. **Theme parity**: cycle Ivory → Dark → Mono Clean. Card hover shadow may not appear in Dark (expected); skeleton placeholders should be visible in all three.
174	8. **Tag cap**: find an extension with ≥4 tags (or seed one) — confirm only 3 pills render with `+N` indicator.
175	9. **Mobile**: collapse viewport to 375px — ResultsSummary chips should wrap; FilterBar pickers should already wrap (existing behavior preserved).
176	10. **Type-check + lint**: `bun run typecheck && bun run lint` per `docs/plan.md` validate pipeline.
177	11. **Coverage**: no logic changes — existing tests should pass without modification. ResultsSummary is presentational; chip-dismiss behavior is exercised through `useFilters` which is already covered.
178	
179	## Out of scope
180	
181	- Pagination polish, infinite scroll
182	- Density toggle (list vs. grid view)
183	- Card SaveButton / favorites surfacing on hover
184	- Filter persistence across sessions
185	- Sidebar refinement
186	- Detail page changes
187	
```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Building2, Download, Star } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	import { deptPath, MY_DEPT_ID } from "~~/shared/data/departments"
4	import { tagLabel } from "~~/shared/tags"
5	import type { ExtensionListItem } from "~~/shared/db/queries-types"
6	import type { Locale } from "~~/shared/types"
7	
8	const props = defineProps<{ ext: ExtensionListItem }>()
9	
10	const { locale } = useI18n()
11	const localePath = useLocalePath()
12	
13	const TAG_VISIBLE_LIMIT = 3
14	
15	const BADGE_LABEL = { official: "Official", popular: "Popular", new: "New" } as const
16	const BADGE_CLASS = {
17	  official: "badge-official",
18	  popular: "badge-popular",
19	  new: "badge-new",
20	} as const
21	
22	const name = computed(() =>
23	  locale.value === "zh" && props.ext.nameZh ? props.ext.nameZh : props.ext.name,
24	)
25	const desc = computed(() =>
26	  locale.value === "zh" && props.ext.descriptionZh
27	    ? props.ext.descriptionZh
28	    : props.ext.description,
29	)
30	
31	const deptTrail = computed(() =>
32	  props.ext.deptId ? deptPath(props.ext.deptId, locale.value as Locale) : null,
33	)
34	const deptLeaf = computed(() =>
35	  deptTrail.value && deptTrail.value.length > 0
36	    ? deptTrail.value[deptTrail.value.length - 1]
37	    : null,
38	)
39	const isMine = computed(() => {
40	  const id = props.ext.deptId
41	  if (!id) return false
42	  return id === MY_DEPT_ID || id.startsWith(`${MY_DEPT_ID}.`)
43	})
44	
45	const visibleTags = computed(() => props.ext.tagIds.slice(0, TAG_VISIBLE_LIMIT))

50	function formatCount(n: number): string {
51	  if (n < 1000) return String(n)
52	  return `${(n / 1000).toFixed(n >= 100000 ? 0 : 1)}k`
53	}
54	</script>

{
  "file_path": "/Users/hutusi/.claude/plans/let-s-take-a-look-sequential-beaver.md",
  "content": "# Extension list page — elegance pass\n\n## Context\n\nRound 1 (already shipped on `feat/ext-list-elegance`, commits 9adb8be..08a1b5c) tightened ExtCard typography, introduced ResultsSummary with dismissible filter chips, relocated SortSelect, softened FilterBar's border, and added ExtGridSkeleton. After review, three issues remain:\n\n1. **The horizontal divider under FilterBar still reads as a hard rule** — even at 60% opacity, the line breaks the editorial flow.\n2. **ExtCard still carries too many elements** — the tag pills row and the inline Install button compete with the name and description. The card should read as a preview, not an action panel.\n3. **The filter rail at the top is still chunky** — three rows of pill buttons + bordered popover boxes + a redundant quick-filter row (Trending/New/Official/Open Source) make the rail feel utilitarian.\n\nRound 2 goes further: cards lose tags + Install (becoming wider-than-tall rectangles naturally as rows fall away), the FilterBar collapses to a single quieter pill row with no border, and the redundant FilterChips row goes away entirely. Sort and dismissible-chip state remain in ResultsSummary as shipped in round 1.\n\n## Round 1 (shipped — reference only)\n\n- C1 `9adb8be` — type scale collapsed to 4 sizes; dept moved below desc as text; tag cap +N indicator; size-11 icon; hover shadow\n- C2 `cff0aea` — ResultsSummary with dismissible chips; Clear all link; count moves out of header\n- C3 `3fee07a` — SortSelect to ResultsSummary; Scope/Sort labels dropped; FilterBar border softened to 60%\n- C4 `08a1b5c` — ExtGridSkeleton for first paint\n\n## Approach (round 2)\n\nThree more commits on `feat/ext-list-elegance`. Each independently reviewable.\n\n### Commit 5 — `refactor(ext-list): simplify ExtCard to a preview rectangle`\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue`\n\nChanges:\n\n- **Remove the tag pills row entirely** — drop the `<div v-if=\"ext.tagIds.length > 0\">` block, the `visibleTags` / `hiddenTagCount` computeds, and the `tagLabel` + `TAG_VISIBLE_LIMIT` imports. Tag *filtering* is unaffected; cards just stop displaying them. Discovery happens via search + sidebar taxonomy + the active-chip row.\n- **Remove the InstallButton** from the footer. The whole-card `NuxtLink` already targets the detail page (via the `after:absolute after:inset-0` pseudo-element); install lives on the detail page. Drop the `<InstallButton>` markup and the `relative z-10` wrapper.\n- **Consolidate footer to a single bullet-separated meta line**: `🏢 Dept · ★ rating · ⬇ downloads`. All three on the same row, separated by middle dots, all in `text-[12px]` muted ink (rating + count stay font-mono for digit alignment). Remove the separate dept block above; dept now lives on the meta line.\n- **Spacing adjustment** — drop the card's internal `gap-3` to `gap-2` and `p-[18px]` to `p-4` so the now-shorter card doesn't feel padded. The card will end up roughly 280×130px on desktop instead of 280×220px — a natural wide rectangle.\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue`\n\n- Drop the tag-pill placeholder row and the install-button placeholder bar\n- Match new dimensions and spacing so the skeleton silhouette matches the simplified card\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtGrid.vue`\n\n- Keep the existing `grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))] gap-4` — auto-fill at 280px gives 3–4 columns on a standard desktop, which the user has confirmed is the preferred density. The card's new rectangular shape comes from removing rows, not from changing column count.\n\n### Commit 6 — `refactor(ext-list): quiet FilterBar to a single pill row`\n\nVisual goal: keep pills + popover triggers (the user opted for \"quiet pills\" over text-link rails), but lighten contrast, drop the prefix labels, and collapse to a single row. Border-bottom goes away entirely.\n\n**File:** `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue`\n\n- Drop `border-b border-(--color-border)/60 pb-3` entirely; replace with `mb-6` whitespace only — no rule.\n- Collapse to a single `flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2` row containing: `<ScopePills /> <DeptPicker /> <CreatorPicker /> <PublisherPicker /> <TagDrawer />`.\n- TagDrawer's *trigger* now sits inline with the other controls; its *panel* (when open) still expands below the rail as it does today.\n- Remove the `<FilterChips />` reference (deletion comes in commit 7).\n\n**File:** `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue`\n\n- Lighten the active state: replace `bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-bg) border-(--color-ink)` (full inversion) with `bg-(--color-ink)/8 text-(--color-ink) border-(--color-ink)/25 font-semibold` — a tinted, outlined pill rather than a filled one.\n- Keep inactive style as-is (`border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted)` with hover bump to ink).\n- Reduce padding from `px-3 py-1` to `px-2.5 py-0.5` so the row reads as a single line of compact pills.\n\n**Files:** `app/components/filters/DeptPicker.vue`, `app/components/filters/CreatorPicker.vue`, `app/components/filters/PublisherPicker.vue`\n\n- Drop the `<span class=\"font-semibold\">{{ t(\"filters.dept.label\") }}:</span>` prefix from each trigger (was \"Department:\", \"Creator:\", \"Publisher:\"). The popover trigger now shows the category name *when no selection is active* (e.g., `Dept ▾`) or the selected value *when set* (e.g., `Eng / Cloud ▾`).\n- Reduce trigger padding to `px-2.5 py-0.5` and font to `text-[12px]` to match the new compact pill row.\n- Soften the active border from `border-(--color-ink)/35` to `border-(--color-ink)/20`.\n- Selected-state still uses bold text + ink color to mark \"this filter is set\" — the bold weight does the work the border used to.\n\n**File:** `app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue`\n\n- Trigger button: match the new compact pill style (`px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px]`).\n- The expandable panel below stays unchanged (multi-select tag pills, match-mode toggle, clear-tags link all still work as-is).\n- Keep the accent-tinted count badge when tags are active — it's allowed by decision #11 (filter-active state).\n\n### Commit 7 — `refactor(ext-list): drop FilterChips row`\n\n**Files:**\n\n- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — remove `<FilterChips />` reference (already handled in commit 6's reshape; commit 7 finalizes the cleanup if any reference remains).\n- `app/components/filters/FilterChips.vue` — delete the file.\n\nRationale:\n\n- \"Trending\" / \"New\" overlap with the sort dropdown (Stars / Downloads / Recent already cover the temporal axis).\n- \"Official\" is already visible as a gold badge on every official card — the filter chip is redundant with the visual signal.\n- \"Open Source\" / \"Free\" is rarely the differentiator for a tools marketplace; if it becomes one, add as a toggle in ResultsSummary later.\n\nThe `filter` URL param still validates against the schema — old bookmarked URLs (`?filter=trending`) keep working and surface as a dismissible chip in ResultsSummary.
