## Approved Plan:
# Extension list page — elegance pass

## Context

Round 1 (already shipped on `feat/ext-list-elegance`, commits 9adb8be..08a1b5c) tightened ExtCard typography, introduced ResultsSummary with dismissible filter chips, relocated SortSelect, softened FilterBar's border, and added ExtGridSkeleton. After review, three issues remain:

1. **The horizontal divider under FilterBar still reads as a hard rule** — even at 60% opacity, the line breaks the editorial flow.
2. **ExtCard still carries too many elements** — the tag pills row and the inline Install button compete with the name and description. The card should read as a preview, not an action panel.
3. **The filter rail at the top is still chunky** — three rows of pill buttons + bordered popover boxes + a redundant quick-filter row (Trending/New/Official/Open Source) make the rail feel utilitarian.

Round 2 goes further: cards lose tags + Install (becoming wider-than-tall rectangles naturally as rows fall away), the FilterBar collapses to a single quieter pill row with no border, and the redundant FilterChips row goes away entirely. Sort and dismissible-chip state remain in ResultsSummary as shipped in round 1.

## Round 1 (shipped — reference only)

- C1 `9adb8be` — type scale collapsed to 4 sizes; dept moved below desc as text; tag cap +N indicator; size-11 icon; hover shadow
- C2 `cff0aea` — ResultsSummary with dismissible chips; Clear all link; count moves out of header
- C3 `3fee07a` — SortSelect to ResultsSummary; Scope/Sort labels dropped; FilterBar border softened to 60%
- C4 `08a1b5c` — ExtGridSkeleton for first paint

## Approach (round 2)

Three more commits on `feat/ext-list-elegance`. Each independently reviewable.

### Commit 5 — `refactor(ext-list): simplify ExtCard to a preview rectangle`

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue`

Changes:

- **Remove the tag pills row entirely** — drop the `<div v-if="ext.tagIds.length > 0">` block, the `visibleTags` / `hiddenTagCount` computeds, and the `tagLabel` + `TAG_VISIBLE_LIMIT` imports. Tag *filtering* is unaffected; cards just stop displaying them. Discovery happens via search + sidebar taxonomy + the active-chip row.
- **Remove the InstallButton** from the footer. The whole-card `NuxtLink` already targets the detail page (via the `after:absolute after:inset-0` pseudo-element); install lives on the detail page. Drop the `<InstallButton>` markup and the `relative z-10` wrapper.
- **Consolidate footer to a single bullet-separated meta line**: `🏢 Dept · ★ rating · ⬇ downloads`. All three on the same row, separated by middle dots, all in `text-[12px]` muted ink (rating + count stay font-mono for digit alignment). Remove the separate dept block above; dept now lives on the meta line.
- **Spacing adjustment** — drop the card's internal `gap-3` to `gap-2` and `p-[18px]` to `p-4` so the now-shorter card doesn't feel padded. The card will end up roughly 280×130px on desktop instead of 280×220px — a natural wide rectangle.

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue`

- Drop the tag-pill placeholder row and the install-button placeholder bar
- Match new dimensions and spacing so the skeleton silhouette matches the simplified card

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtGrid.vue`

- Keep the existing `grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(280px,1fr))] gap-4` — auto-fill at 280px gives 3–4 columns on a standard desktop, which the user has confirmed is the preferred density. The card's new rectangular shape comes from removing rows, not from changing column count.

### Commit 6 — `refactor(ext-list): quiet FilterBar to a single pill row`

Visual goal: keep pills + popover triggers (the user opted for "quiet pills" over text-link rails), but lighten contrast, drop the prefix labels, and collapse to a single row. Border-bottom goes away entirely.

**File:** `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue`

- Drop `border-b border-(--color-border)/60 pb-3` entirely; replace with `mb-6` whitespace only — no rule.
- Collapse to a single `flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2` row containing: `<ScopePills /> <DeptPicker /> <CreatorPicker /> <PublisherPicker /> <TagDrawer />`.
- TagDrawer's *trigger* now sits inline with the other controls; its *panel* (when open) still expands below the rail as it does today.
- Remove the `<FilterChips />` reference (deletion comes in commit 7).

**File:** `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue`

- Lighten the active state: replace `bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-bg) border-(--color-ink)` (full inversion) with `bg-(--color-ink)/8 text-(--color-ink) border-(--color-ink)/25 font-semibold` — a tinted, outlined pill rather than a filled one.
- Keep inactive style as-is (`border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted)` with hover bump to ink).
- Reduce padding from `px-3 py-1` to `px-2.5 py-0.5` so the row reads as a single line of compact pills.

**Files:** `app/components/filters/DeptPicker.vue`, `app/components/filters/CreatorPicker.vue`, `app/components/filters/PublisherPicker.vue`

- Drop the `<span class="font-semibold">{{ t("filters.dept.label") }}:</span>` prefix from each trigger (was "Department:", "Creator:", "Publisher:"). The popover trigger now shows the category name *when no selection is active* (e.g., `Dept ▾`) or the selected value *when set* (e.g., `Eng / Cloud ▾`).
- Reduce trigger padding to `px-2.5 py-0.5` and font to `text-[12px]` to match the new compact pill row.
- Soften the active border from `border-(--color-ink)/35` to `border-(--color-ink)/20`.
- Selected-state still uses bold text + ink color to mark "this filter is set" — the bold weight does the work the border used to.

**File:** `app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue`

- Trigger button: match the new compact pill style (`px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px]`).
- The expandable panel below stays unchanged (multi-select tag pills, match-mode toggle, clear-tags link all still work as-is).
- Keep the accent-tinted count badge when tags are active — it's allowed by decision #11 (filter-active state).

### Commit 7 — `refactor(ext-list): drop FilterChips row`

**Files:**

- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — remove `<FilterChips />` reference (already handled in commit 6's reshape; commit 7 finalizes the cleanup if any reference remains).
- `app/components/filters/FilterChips.vue` — delete the file.

Rationale:

- "Trending" / "New" overlap with the sort dropdown (Stars / Downloads / Recent already cover the temporal axis).
- "Official" is already visible as a gold badge on every official card — the filter chip is redundant with the visual signal.
- "Open Source" / "Free" is rarely the differentiator for a tools marketplace; if it becomes one, add as a toggle in ResultsSummary later.

The `filter` URL param still validates against the schema — old bookmarked URLs (`?filter=trending`) keep working and surface as a dismissible chip in ResultsSummary. We just stop *setting* it from the UI.

i18n keys (`filters.chips.*`) are left in place — they cost nothing and the dismissible chip still uses them.

## Critical files

**Edited:**

- `app/components/extension/ExtCard.vue` — commit 5
- `app/components/extension/ExtGridSkeleton.vue` — commit 5
- `app/components/filters/FilterBar.vue` — commit 6 (and final cleanup in 7)
- `app/components/filters/ScopePills.vue` — commit 6
- `app/components/filters/DeptPicker.vue` — commit 6
- `app/components/filters/CreatorPicker.vue` — commit 6
- `app/components/filters/PublisherPicker.vue` — commit 6
- `app/components/filters/TagDrawer.vue` — commit 6

**Deleted:**

- `app/components/filters/FilterChips.vue` — commit 7

**Untouched (verified still needed):**

- `app/pages/extensions/index.vue` — header + ResultsSummary + ExtGrid wiring stays as round 1 shipped
- `app/components/extension/ResultsSummary.vue` — the dismissible `filter` chip stays in place for old-URL forward compat

## Reused utilities (round 2)

- `formatCount()` and `deptPath()` / `MY_DEPT_ID` — all still used by ExtCard's simplified meta line
- `useFilters()` — unchanged
- CSS vars (`--color-ink`, `--color-ink-muted`, `--color-border`, `--color-accent`, `--color-card`, `--radius-card`) — unchanged

## Accent budget

Post-round-2, the list page's accent moments are:

- ✅ Card hover border tint (unchanged)
- ✅ TagDrawer active filter count badge (unchanged)
- ✅ "View Panorama" link (unchanged)
- ✅ Dept "Mine" emphasis on cards (text-only accent, kept from round 1)
- ❌ ResultsSummary chips: neutral
- ❌ Scope pills: now ink-tinted active state, no accent

**The InstallButton is gone from the list page** — install only exists on detail pages now. This removes the strongest accent footprint from the list. Net: significantly less accent ink on the page, which fits decision #11's "one intentional accent moment per viewport" goal.

## Verification

1. **Local dev**: `bun dev` → open `http://localhost:3000/en/extensions`.
2. **Card structure**: confirm cards show icon + name + badge on the header row, 2-line description, and a single meta line (`🏢 Dept · ★ N.N · ⬇ count`). No tag pills, no Install button on the card.
3. **Whole-card click**: clicking anywhere on a card body navigates to the detail page where InstallButton still exists.
4. **Filter bar**: confirm the rail is a single line of compact pills (Scope pills + Dept/Creator/Publisher/Tags triggers). No horizontal rule below.
5. **Tag drawer**: clicking "Tags ▾" still expands the multi-select panel; selecting tags still adds them as filters and dismissible chips appear in ResultsSummary.
6. **FilterChips gone**: no Trending/New/Official/Free pill row anywhere. URL `/en/extensions?filter=trending` still loads cleanly; the dismissible chip in ResultsSummary still appears (forward compat).
7. **Picker trigger labels**: when no selection, triggers show category name only (`Dept ▾`); when a selection is active, they show the selected value (`Eng / Cloud ▾`) in bold.
8. **Locale parity**: switch to `/zh/extensions` — card meta line and pickers render in Chinese.
9. **Theme parity**: Ivory → Dark → Mono Clean — all three should render the new lighter pill active state without darkness/contrast issues. Dark theme's lighter scope pill (`bg-ink/8`) may need an opacity bump (`/15` or `/20`) — adjust if needed during implementation.
10. **Skeleton**: hard-reload `/en/extensions` — skeleton silhouette should match the new card (no tag bars, no install bar, single meta line).
11. **Validate**: `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test`.

## Out of scope

- Pagination polish, infinite scroll
- Density toggle (list vs. grid view)
- Card SaveButton / favorites surfacing on hover
- Filter persistence across sessions
- Sidebar refinement
- Detail page changes

```

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { ChevronDown } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\nimport type { Locale, TagFacet } from \"~~/shared/types\"\nimport { tagLabel } from \"~~/shared/tags\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  tags: TagFacet[]\n}>()\n\nconst { t, locale } = useI18n()\nconst { filters, update } = useFilters()\n\nconst TAG_COLLAPSE_LIMIT = 14\n\nconst open = ref(false)\nconst showAll = ref(false)\n\nconst activeTags = computed<string[]>(() => filters.value.tags ?? [])\nconst matchMode = computed(() => filters.value.tagMatch ?? \"any\")\n\nconst visibleTags = computed(() => {\n  if (showAll.value) return props.tags\n  return props.tags.slice(0, TAG_COLLAPSE_LIMIT)\n})\nconst hiddenCount = computed(() => Math.max(0, props.tags.length - TAG_COLLAPSE_LIMIT))\n\nfunction toggleTag(id: string) {\n  const set = new Set(activeTags.value)\n  if (set.has(id)) set.delete(id)\n  else set.add(id)\n  const next = Array.from(set)\n  update({ tags: next.length ? next : undefined })\n}\n\nfunction setMatch(mode: \"any\" | \"all\") {\n  update({ tagMatch: mode === \"any\" ? undefined : mode })\n}\n\nfunction clearTags() {\n  update({ tags: undefined, tagMatch: undefined })\n}\n\nfunction labelFor(id: string): string {\n  return tagLabel(id, locale.value as Locale)\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div class=\"contents\">\n    <button\n      type=\"button\"\n      class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-2.5 py-0.5 text-[12px] transition-colors\"\n      :class=\"activeTags.length\n        ? 'border-(--color-ink)/20 bg-(--color-card) text-(--color-ink) font-semibold'\n        : 'border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'\"\n      :aria-expanded=\"open\"\n      @click=\"open = !open\"\n    >\n      <span>{{ t(\"filters.tagsToggle\") }}</span>\n      <span\n        v-if=\"activeTags.length\"\n        class=\"rounded-full bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg) font-mono text-[10px] px-1.5 py-0.5\"\n      >\n        {{ activeTags.length }}\n      </span>\n      <ChevronDown\n        :size=\"12\"\n        :class=\"['transition-transform', open ? '' : '-rotate-90']\"\n        aria-hidden=\"true\"\n      />\n    </button>\n\n    <div\n      v-if=\"open\"\n      class=\"basis-full mt-1 rounded-md border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-3\"\n    >\n      <div class=\"flex flex-wrap gap-1.5\">\n        <button\n          v-for=\"tag in visibleTags\"\n          :key=\"tag.id\"\n          type=\"button\"\n          class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded-full border px-2 py-0.5 text-[11px] transition-colors\"\n          :class=\"activeTags.includes(tag.id)\n            ? 'border-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg)'\n            : 'border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'\"\n          @click=\"toggleTag(tag.id)\"\n        >\n          <span>{{ labelFor(tag.id) }}</span>\n          <span class=\"font-mono text-[10px] opacity-70\">{{ tag.count }}</span>\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      <div v-if=\"hiddenCount > 0\" class=\"mt-3 flex justify-center\">\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          class=\"text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)\"\n          @click=\"showAll = !showAll\"\n        >\n          {{ showAll ? t(\"filters.showLess\") : t(\"filters.showMoreCount\", { count: hiddenCount }) }}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n\n      <div\n        v-if=\"activeTags.length\"\n        class=\"mt-3 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-2 border-t border-(--color-border) pt-3\"\n      >\n        <div class=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n          <span>{{ t(\"filters.matchModeGroupLabel\") }}:</span>\n          <div class=\"inline-flex overflow-hidden rounded-md border border-(--color-border)\">\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              class=\"px-2 py-0.5 transition-colors\"\n              :class=\"matchMode === 'any'\n                ? 'bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg)'\n                : 'text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'\"\n              @click=\"setMatch('any')\"\n            >\n              {{ t(\"filters.tags.any\") }}\n            </button>\n            <button\n              type=\"button\"\n              class=\"px-2 py-0.5 transition-colors border-l border-(--color-border)\"\n              :class=\"matchMode === 'all'\n                ? 'bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg)'\n                : 'text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)'\"\n              @click=\"setMatch('all')\"\n            >\n              {{ t(\"filters.tags.all\") }}\n            </button>\n          </div>\n        </div>\n        <button\n          type=\"button\"\n          class=\"text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink)\"\n          @click=\"clearTags\"\n        >\n          {{ t(\"filters.tags.clear\") }}\n        </button>\n      </div>\n    </div>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

All 7 tasks completed; no new tasks needed. Round 2 done.

Round 2 added three commits on `feat/ext-list-elegance`:

- **`refactor(ext-list): simplify ExtCard to a preview rectangle`** (724d183) — dropped the tag pills row and the inline InstallButton; consolidated dept/stars/downloads onto a single bullet-separated meta line; tightened padding/gap so the card reads as a wider-than-tall preview, not an action panel.
- **`refactor(ext-list): quiet FilterBar to a single pill row`** (93ba9be) — removed the horizontal divider entirely; collapsed all controls into one flex rail; lightened ScopePills's active state from full inversion to a tinted outline (`bg-ink/8`, `border-ink/25`); dropped the "Department:/Creator:/Publisher:" prefix labels so triggers show only the active value (or category name when empty); TagDrawer's expanded panel wraps to a new row via `display:contents + basis-full`.
- **`refactor(ext-list): drop FilterChips component`** (b938ebb) — removed the redundant All/Trending/New/Official/Open Source pill row and deleted the component. Old URLs with `?filter=trending` still parse and surface as dismissible chips in ResultsSummary.

Verified: lint clean, 250/250 tests pass, dev server returns 200, Install button absent from cards, no `#tag` pills on cards, no FilterChips row in markup, lightened pill classes (`bg-(--color-ink)/8`, `border-(--color-ink)/20`) confirmed in active-filter URLs.

> DEVELOPER

I think the cards should be bigger, and the internal padding needs to be larger. what do you think?

> AGENT

I think you're right — after removing tags + install button, the cards may now feel cramped at `p-4` (16px). My read:

- **Padding**: bump `p-4` → `p-5` (20px) or `p-6` (24px). At `p-6` cards feel deliberately spacious.
- **Min card width**: keep ≥3 columns (you confirmed earlier). Going from `minmax(280px)` to `minmax(320–360px)` shifts most desktop layouts from 4 cards/row to 3 cards/row, so each card gets wider.
- **Internal gap**: bump `gap-2` (8px) → `gap-3` (12px) so the three rows breathe.
- **Description**: with more height available, could grow from 2 lines to 3 lines.

Let me confirm the dial before changing anything.

Perfect. Now I have enough information. Let me create a comprehensive summary of my findings.

## Extension Detail Page Exploration Report

I've conducted a thorough investigation of the extension detail page architecture in the AgentCenter Nuxt app. Here are my findings organized by the six categories you requested:

---

### 1. Detail Page Route File

**Path:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/pages/extensions/[slug].vue`

**Structure & Composition:**
- 42 lines total
- Fetches extension detail data from `/api/internal/extension-detail` (query-based, slug computed)
- Handles 404 on missing extension (line 12-14)
- Computes i18n-aware `name`, `description`, and `publishedAt`
- Computes `shareUrl` for social sharing
- Defines OG image with bundled Frame template
- **Main layout (lines 45-79):** Two-column grid
  - `lg:grid-cols-[1fr_300px]` — main content (flexible) + 300px fixed sidebar
  - `gap-8` between columns
  - `px-6 py-8 max-w-7xl` outer container

**Child components in sequence:**
1. **ExtHero** — Title, description, rating, downloads, department, action buttons (Install/Save/Share)
2. **ExtTabs** — Three-tab interface: Overview (readme), Setup (install command), Permissions
3. **ExtAboutCard** (sidebar) — License, scope, published date, homepage & repo links
4. **ExtRelatedList** (sidebar) — Recommended extensions with icons

---

### 2. Detail-Page Components

| Component | Path | Purpose |
|-----------|------|---------|
| **ExtHero** | `app/components/extension/ExtHero.vue` | Hero header with icon (14px emoji in 56px container), h1 title (`font-serif text-3xl`), description, star/download badges, department trail, action button strip (66 lines) |
| **ExtTabs** | `app/components/extension/ExtTabs.vue` | Tab strip (reka-ui Tabs) with three triggers: Overview/Setup/Permissions; Overview renders Markdown + tag pills; Setup shows install command; Permissions lists permission keys (75 lines) |
| **InstallButton** | `app/components/extension/InstallButton.vue` | Primary CTA button; `text-[12px]` default or `text-[11px]` when `size="sm"`; dual-size support via computed `sizeClass`; states: default (accent bg), loading (opacity-60), installed (accent/10 bg + checkmark) (58 lines) |
| **SaveButton** | `app/components/extension/SaveButton.vue` | Secondary bookmark button; two variants: `pill` (with label) and `icon`; on detail page used as `pill` (no variant prop); 3s toast feedback on save (58 lines) |
| **ShareButton** | `app/components/extension/ShareButton.vue` | Uses Web Share API with fallback to clipboard; 1.4s toast on copy; bordered neutral style (48 lines) |
| **ExtAboutCard** | `app/components/extension/ExtAboutCard.vue` | Side panel card (`rounded-(--radius-card) border bg-(--color-card) p-5`); h2 "About" header (`text-sm font-semibold`); dl with 3 items (license, scope, published); separator + links (homepage/repo) with icons (79 lines) |
| **ExtRelatedList** | `app/components/extension/ExtRelatedList.vue` | Side panel card (same styling as ExtAboutCard); h2 "Related" header; ul of NuxtLinks with emoji + name + truncate (40 lines) |
| **Markdown** | `app/components/Markdown.vue` | README renderer; markdown-it + DOMPurify sanitization; renders to `prose prose-sm max-w-none` div; allows h1-h6, p, lists, code/pre, tables, links, img, blockquote; strips class attrs but allows href/src/alt (31 lines) |
| **InstallCommand** | `app/components/extension/InstallCommand.vue` | Code block in terminal dark theme (`bg-(--color-ink) text-(--color-bg) p-3`); shows `$ agentcenter install {slug}`; copy button with feedback (38 lines) |

---

### 3. Server Endpoint & Response Shape

**Path:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/server/api/internal/extension-detail.get.ts`

**Response shape:**
```typescript
{
  ext: Extension,           // Main extension record
  versions: Version[],      // Version history (fetched but not used on detail page currently)
  related: RelatedRow[]     // 3-5 recommended extensions with id/slug/name/nameZh/iconEmoji/iconColor
}
```

**Data flow:**
- Query param: `slug` (string, required)
- Queries:
  - `extensionsRepo.findBySlug(db, slug)` → full extension
  - `versionsRepo.listForExtension(db, ext.id)` → versions
  - `extensionsRepo.findRelated(db, ext.id, ext.category)` → related by category
- Error handling: 404 if not found, 400 if slug missing

**Extension properties accessed on detail page:**
- Identity: `id`, `slug`
- Editorial: `name`, `nameZh`, `description`, `descriptionZh`, `iconEmoji`, `iconColor`
- Metadata: `licenseSpdx`, `scope`, `publishedAt`, `homepageUrl`, `repoUrl`, `deptId`
- Content: `readmeMd` (markdown string, server-rendered on detail page)
- Tags: `tagIds` (array of string IDs, used in tags section of Overview tab)
- Permissions: `permissions` (object, keys indicate true permissions; null if none)
- Stats: `starsAvg` (string, avg rating), `downloadsCount` (number)

---

### 4. README Rendering Pipeline

**Markdown Component:** `/Users/hutusi/workspace/ai/naive/agentcenter-nuxt/app/components/Markdown.vue`

**Pipeline:**
1. **Input:** `source` prop (raw markdown string from `ext.readmeMd`)
2. **Parse:** `MarkdownIt` instance (lines 7-12)
   - `html: false` — no raw HTML pass-through
   - `linkify: true` — auto-link URLs
   - `breaks: false` — no `\n` → `<br>` conversion
   - `typographer: true` — smart quotes/dashes
3. **Render:** `md.render(source)` → raw HTML
4. **Sanitize:** `DOMPurify.sanitize()` with allowlist (lines 16-24)
   - **Allowed tags:** h1–h6, p, a, ul, ol, li, strong, em, code, pre, blockquote, hr, br, img, table (thead/tbody/tr/th/td), span, div
   - **Allowed attributes:** href, title, alt, src, class
   - **URI whitelist:** https://, http://, mailto:, relative (/, ../)
5. **Output:** `v-html` into `<div class="prose prose-sm max-w-none text-(--color-ink)">`

**Location:** Browser-side (Markdown.vue is an app component, rendering happens client-side). Note: CLAUDE.md §6 says "server-rendered with markdown-it + DOMPurify" but implementation is client-side. The markdown string itself is fetched as-is from the server; rendering happens in the browser.

---

---

### 6. Visual Hierarchy & Frictions Observed

#### **Font & Spacing Consistency:**
- **Hero title:** `font-serif text-3xl` (Fraunces, largest on page)
- **Card headers:** `text-sm font-semibold` (ExtAboutCard, ExtRelatedList h2)
- **Body text:** Mixed: `text-sm` (tabs), `text-[13px]` (ExtCard description), `text-[12px]` (stats), `text-[11px]` (tag pills)
- **Friction:** No unified body size scale. List page uses `text-[15px]` for card titles (ExtCard), `text-[13px]` for description. Detail hero uses `font-serif text-3xl`. Sidebar uses `text-sm` everywhere.

#### **Layout & Density:**
- **Main container:** `px-6 py-8 max-w-7xl` — matches browse page
- **Hero spacing:** Hero is `mb-6` from ExtTabs, which starts `mt-4` in TabsContent (only 10px gap between sections)
- **Sidebar:** Fixed 300px width, `gap-8` from main. Both cards in sidebar use `p-5` interior padding.
- **Friction:** Sidebar panels (ExtAboutCard, ExtRelatedList) feel dense; `p-5` + `text-sm` + `space-y-2` is tight. Compare to ExtCard which has `p-5` but slightly more breathing room with larger font sizes.

#### **Accent Usage:**
- **Hero hero buttons:** InstallButton is `bg-(--color-accent) text-(--color-accent-fg)` — CORRECT per decision #11 (primary CTA)
- **SaveButton:** `border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card)` default, shifts to `border-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-accent)/10` when saved — CORRECT (secondary, accent only on active state)
- **ShareButton:** Neutral `border-(--color-border)` throughout — CORRECT (tertiary)
- **ExtAboutCard links:** `hover:text-(--color-accent)` — CORRECT (hover accent)
- **Friction:** None here; restraint is well-maintained.

#### **Mismatch: List vs. Detail Editorial Feel:**
- **List page (ExtCard):**
  - Card background: white (`bg-(--color-card)`)
  - Border: subtle `border-(--color-border)`
  - Hover: `-translate-y-0.5` + shadow + `border-(--color-accent)/30`
  - Icon: 44px box, `text-[18px]` emoji
  - Title: `text-[15px] font-semibold` (compact, sans-serif)
  - Description: `text-[13px]` with `-webkit-line-clamp: 3`
  - Stats: `text-[12px] font-mono`
  
- **Detail page (ExtHero + sidebar):**
  - Hero icon: 56px box, `text-[24px]` emoji
  - Hero title: `font-serif text-3xl` (serif, editorial)
  - Hero description: `text-(--color-ink-muted)` (secondary color, large)
  - Hero stats: `text-sm` (larger than list)
  - Sidebar: white cards with `p-5`, `text-sm` body
  - Overall: More editorial, serif-heavy, spacious hero; compact sidebar

- **Friction:** The detail page **hero feels editorial and generous** (serif title, large emoji), but the **sidebar panels revert to utilitarian** (`text-sm`, dense `dl`, no visual breathing room). The prose README section uses default `prose` styling which may not match the Ivory aesthetic. No branded border or accent treatment on the readme container.

#### **Button Positioning & Visual Weight:**
- **Action buttons (Install/Save/Share):** Placed directly under hero in 3-button row, above tabs (line 59-63 in ExtHero)
- **Friction:** Buttons are `text-[12px]` and `px-3 py-1.5`, making them **visual weight-neutral** (not prominent). The Install button is accent-colored, so it stands out, but the trio together reads as secondary compared to the large hero title. No clear visual hierarchy suggesting "Install this now" before reading the README. Compare to retail/SaaS patterns where primary CTAs are larger and more positioned.

#### **Tab Styling:**
- **TabsList:** `inline-flex h-9 items-center justify-center gap-1 rounded-md border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-sidebar) p-1`
- **TabsTrigger active:** `bg-(--color-card) text-(--color-ink) shadow-sm`
- **Friction:** Tabs use `bg-(--color-sidebar)` as the container background, which is slightly darker than white. When a tab is active, it "pops" to white. This works but feels subtle; the color change is ~1.5% lightness shift (97.5% → 98.5%). No font-weight change on active, just color + shadow.

#### **Related Extensions Block:**
- **Styling:** Simple NuxtLink list with emoji + name, `hover:text-(--color-accent)`, `truncate` on name
- **Friction:** No visual distinction between related items (no cards, no icons, no metadata). Reads as plain list compared to the feature-rich ExtCard on the browse page. Could feel like an afterthought.

#### **Missing Visual Patterns:**
- **README container:** No border, no background tint, no distinction from white background. Just `prose` + color. Could integrate better with the Ivory aesthetic if it had a subtle card or border.
- **Breadcrumb:** No breadcrumb or back-link visible on detail page; if user lands from off-site, navigation back to browse is not obvious at first glance.
- **Copy/Share feedback:** Transient toast messages (1.4–3s) are not visible; no UI indication success feedback was registered (common UX friction).

#### **Typography Metrics Summary:**

| Element | Detail Page | List Page |
|---------|------------|-----------|
| Primary title | `font-serif text-3xl` | `text-[15px] font-semibold` |
| Description | `text-base` implicit | `text-[13px]` |
| Stats | `text-sm` | `text-[12px] font-mono` |
| Sidebar headers | `text-sm font-semibold` | N/A |
| Body/dl | `text-sm` | N/A |
| Tags | `text-[11px] font-semibold` | N/A |

**Unified scale recommendation would be:** Consider a defined scale (e.g., `text-xs: 11px, text-sm: 13px, text-base: 15px, text-lg: 18px, text-xl: 24px, text-3xl: 32px`) and ensure detail sidebar uses `text-base` or `text-sm` consistently, not a mix with custom `text-[Xpx]` values.

---

### Summary of Key File Locations

| Purpose | Path | Lines |
|---------|------|-------|
| Detail page route | `app/pages/extensions/[slug].vue` | 80 |
| Hero section | `app/components/extension/ExtHero.vue` | 66 |
| Tab strip | `app/components/extension/ExtTabs.vue` | 75 |
| Sidebar about card | `app/components/extension/ExtAboutCard.vue` | 79 |
| Related extensions | `app/components/extension/ExtRelatedList.vue` | 40 |
| Install button | `app/components/extension/InstallButton.vue` | 58 |
| Markdown renderer | `app/components/Markdown.vue` | 31 |
| Server endpoint | `server/api/internal/extension-detail.get.ts` | 19 |
| Theme variables | `app/assets/css/tailwind.css` | 91 |
| i18n keys | `i18n/locales/en.json` | (excerpt) |

This structure is well-modularized and clear, with coherent data flow from server → page → child components. The main elegance opportunity is in harmonizing typography scales, tightening the visual relationship between hero and sidebar, and giving the README content stronger editorial treatment to match the serif-heavy, spacious hero aesthetic.

> AGENT

I have enough now. Here's what stands out as elegance gaps:

- **Hero CTA is undersized** — Install/Save/Share are `text-[12px] px-3 py-1.5` (same as on the old list cards). For the page's primary action, they read as secondary. The title is editorial (`font-serif text-3xl`); the buttons aren't.
- **README sits naked** — The main content has no card, no border, no editorial framing. It just floats on the page background. Compare to the now-framed sidebar cards.
- **Sidebar h2s read as spec-sheet** — "About" and "Related" use `text-sm font-semibold` plus dense `dl space-y-2`. With the hero in serif, the sidebar reverts to utilitarian.
- **Tabs are timid** — Active state is `bg-(--color-sidebar)` → `bg-(--color-card)` (a ~1.5% lightness swap). The active tab is barely distinguishable.
- **Related list is anemic** — Plain text-with-emoji list. No badge, no dept, no stars. Reads as afterthought.
- **Stats line uses ASCII `·`** — Hardcoded period before dept trail (`<span>· {{ deptTrail }}</span>`), inconsistent with the proper middle-dot separators the new list card uses.
- **No breadcrumb back to browse** — Users have to use browser back to return to the list.

Let me ask which to tackle.

161	export async function findRelated(
162	  db: Transactable,
163	  extensionId: string,
164	  category: "skills" | "mcp" | "slash" | "plugins",
165	) {
166	  return db
167	    .select(relatedSelect)
168	    .from(extensions)
169	    .where(
170	      and(
171	        eq(extensions.category, category),
172	        eq(extensions.visibility, "published"),
173	        ne(extensions.id, extensionId),
174	      ),
175	    )
176	    .orderBy(desc(extensions.downloadsCount))
177	    .limit(4)
178	}
179	
180	export async function findBySlug(
181	  db: Transactable,
182	  slug: string,
183	  opts: { onlyPublished?: boolean } = {},
184	) {
185	  const onlyPublished = opts.onlyPublished ?? true
186	  const where = onlyPublished
187	    ? and(eq(extensions.slug, slug), eq(extensions.visibility, "published"))
188	    : eq(extensions.slug, slug)
189	
190	  const [row] = await db
191	    .select(detailSelect)
192	    .from(extensions)
193	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
194	    .where(where)
195	    .groupBy(extensions.id)
196	  return row ?? null
197	}
198	
199	export async function findById(db: Transactable, id: string) {
200	  const [row] = await db
201	    .select(detailSelect)
202	    .from(extensions)
203	    .leftJoin(extensionTags, eq(extensionTags.extensionId, extensions.id))
204	    .where(eq(extensions.id, id))

  "content": "# Extension detail page — elegance pass\n\n## Context\n\nThe list page has now had two rounds of refinement on this branch (`feat/ext-list-elegance`, 8 commits shipped: 9adb8be..e01ef18 + 368f0b7 gitignore). Same branch, same PR — several more commits on top of the existing eight.\n\n## Approach\n\nFive commits.\n\n### Commit 9 — `feat(ext-detail): amplify hero with breadcrumb, lede, and size=lg CTA`\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/InstallButton.vue`\n\n- Add a `size=\"lg\"` variant alongside the existing `sm` and default: `px-4 py-2 text-[14px] gap-1.5`. Touch only the `sizeClass` computed and (optionally) the icon size used inside.\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtHero.vue`\n\n- **Title**: `font-serif text-3xl` → `font-serif text-4xl` for stronger anchor on the editorial page.\n- **Description**: bare paragraph → lede: `text-[17px] leading-[1.55] text-(--color-ink-muted) mt-3`.\n- **Icon**: `size-14 text-[24px]` → `size-16 text-[28px]`, matching the larger title.\n- **Stats line**: replace the hardcoded `· ` prefix on the dept span with proper middle-dot separators between every chip — same pattern the new list card uses (`<span class=\"text-(--color-ink-muted)/50\">·</span>` between items). Use a single `text-sm` row.\n- **Action row**: pass `size=\"lg\"` to InstallButton; bump SaveButton + ShareButton to matching `px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px]` so the trio reads as a confident CTA group.\n\n**File:** `app/pages/extensions/[slug].vue`\n\n- Add a subtle breadcrumb above the hero: `<NuxtLink to=\"/extensions\" class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink) mb-4\">← Browse extensions</NuxtLink>`.\n- Use `useLocalePath` so the link respects the active locale.\n\n**Files:** `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json`\n\n- Add `extensions.backToBrowse`: \"← Browse extensions\" / \"← 浏览扩展\".\n\n### Commit 10 — `refactor(ext-detail): frame the README and tab content in a card`\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtTabs.vue`\n\n- Wrap each `<TabsContent>` body in a card div: `rounded-(--radius-card) border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-6 mt-4`. The Overview tab gets the framing it's been missing; Setup and Permissions get matching containers so all three tab states have the same visual weight.\n- Drop the per-element `mt-4` / `mb-2` classes that exist to fake spacing — the card padding handles it now.\n- The tag pills row at the bottom of Overview (`<div v-if=\"tagIds.length > 0\">`) moves inside the same card, with a top divider: `border-t border-(--color-border) pt-4 mt-6`.\n\n**File:** `app/components/Markdown.vue`\n\n- Bump prose scale from `prose prose-sm` to `prose prose-sm` (unchanged) but adjust the wrapper so it works inside a `p-6` card without double-spacing. No structural change; just verify prose first-child top-margin is stripped — it already is via tailwind-typography defaults.\n\n### Commit 11 — `refactor(ext-detail): editorial sidebar pass`\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtAboutCard.vue`\n\n- `h2`: `text-sm font-semibold` → `font-serif text-base font-semibold tracking-tight` (echoes the hero's serif voice in a smaller register).\n- Card padding: `p-5` → `p-6`.\n- `dl` spacing: `space-y-2 text-sm` → `space-y-2.5 text-[13px]`.\n- Links section: bump separator padding from `mt-4 pt-4` → `mt-5 pt-5`; link text size to `text-[13px]`.\n\n**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtRelatedList.vue`\n\n- Match the same serif h2 + p-6 + space-y-3 pattern.\n- Each `<li>` row: enlarge emoji to `text-[18px]` and switch to `flex items-center gap-3` with `py-1` per row for vertical rhythm.\n\n### Commit 12 — `refactor(ext-detail): underline-on-active tabs`\n\n**Files:** `app/components/extension/ExtTabs.vue` plus the underlying primitives if they need adjustment (`app/components/ui/Tabs.vue` / `TabsList.vue` / `TabsTrigger.vue` — check if shadcn-vue exposes these as overridable).\n\n- Drop the `bg-(--color-sidebar)` container and the `bg-(--color-card) shadow-sm` active swap. The current 1.5% lightness contrast is too timid.\n- New design: `TabsList` becomes a row of triggers with `border-b border-(--color-border)` below. ## Approved Plan:
# Extension detail page — elegance pass

## Context

The list page has now had two rounds of refinement on this branch (`feat/ext-list-elegance`, 8 commits shipped: 9adb8be..e01ef18 + 368f0b7 gitignore). The detail page (`/en/extensions/[slug]`) hasn't been touched, and after the list-page work it now reads as mismatched: the hero is editorial-serif but the action buttons are tiny, the README sits naked on the background without framing, the sidebar cards revert to spec-sheet typography, tabs are barely distinguishable when active, and the Related list is anemic.

Goal: bring the detail page in line with the editorial voice of the refined list page. Same branch, same PR — several more commits on top of the existing eight.

## Approach

Five commits.

### Commit 9 — `feat(ext-detail): amplify hero with breadcrumb, lede, and size=lg CTA`

**File:** `app/components/extension/InstallButton.vue`

- Add a `size="lg"` variant alongside the existing `sm` and default: `px-4 py-2 text-[14px] gap-1.5`. Touch only the `sizeClass` computed and (optionally) the icon size used inside.

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtHero.vue`

- **Title**: `font-serif text-3xl` → `font-serif text-4xl` for stronger anchor on the editorial page.
- **Description**: bare paragraph → lede: `text-[17px] leading-[1.55] text-(--color-ink-muted) mt-3`.
- **Icon**: `size-14 text-[24px]` → `size-16 text-[28px]`, matching the larger title.
- **Stats line**: replace the hardcoded `· ` prefix on the dept span with proper middle-dot separators between every chip — same pattern the new list card uses (`<span class="text-(--color-ink-muted)/50">·</span>` between items). Use a single `text-sm` row.
- **Action row**: pass `size="lg"` to InstallButton; bump SaveButton + ShareButton to matching `px-3.5 py-2 text-[13px]` so the trio reads as a confident CTA group.

**File:** `app/pages/extensions/[slug].vue`

- Add a subtle breadcrumb above the hero: `<NuxtLink to="/extensions" class="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-[13px] text-(--color-ink-muted) hover:text-(--color-ink) mb-4">← Browse extensions</NuxtLink>`.
- Use `useLocalePath` so the link respects the active locale.

**Files:** `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json`

- Add `extensions.backToBrowse`: "← Browse extensions" / "← 浏览扩展".

### Commit 10 — `refactor(ext-detail): frame the README and tab content in a card`

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtTabs.vue`

- Wrap each `<TabsContent>` body in a card div: `rounded-(--radius-card) border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-6 mt-4`. The Overview tab gets the framing it's been missing; Setup and Permissions get matching containers so all three tab states have the same visual weight.
- Drop the per-element `mt-4` / `mb-2` classes that exist to fake spacing — the card padding handles it now.
- The tag pills row at the bottom of Overview (`<div v-if="tagIds.length > 0">`) moves inside the same card, with a top divider: `border-t border-(--color-border) pt-4 mt-6`.

**File:** `app/components/Markdown.vue`

- Bump prose scale from `prose prose-sm` to `prose prose-sm` (unchanged) but adjust the wrapper so it works inside a `p-6` card without double-spacing. No structural change; just verify prose first-child top-margin is stripped — it already is via tailwind-typography defaults.

### Commit 11 — `refactor(ext-detail): editorial sidebar pass`

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtAboutCard.vue`

- `h2`: `text-sm font-semibold` → `font-serif text-base font-semibold tracking-tight` (echoes the hero's serif voice in a smaller register).
- Card padding: `p-5` → `p-6`.
- `dl` spacing: `space-y-2 text-sm` → `space-y-2.5 text-[13px]`.
- Links section: bump separator padding from `mt-4 pt-4` → `mt-5 pt-5`; link text size to `text-[13px]`.

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtRelatedList.vue`

- Match the same serif h2 + p-6 + space-y-3 pattern.
- Each `<li>` row: enlarge emoji to `text-[18px]` and switch to `flex items-center gap-3` with `py-1` per row for vertical rhythm.

### Commit 12 — `refactor(ext-detail): underline-on-active tabs`

**Files:** `app/components/extension/ExtTabs.vue` plus the underlying primitives if they need adjustment (`app/components/ui/Tabs.vue` / `TabsList.vue` / `TabsTrigger.vue` — check if shadcn-vue exposes these as overridable).

- Drop the `bg-(--color-sidebar)` container and the `bg-(--color-card) shadow-sm` active swap. The current 1.5% lightness contrast is too timid.
- New design: `TabsList` becomes a row of triggers with `border-b border-(--color-border)` below. Each trigger is `px-3 py-2 text-[14px] font-medium text-(--color-ink-muted) -mb-px border-b-2 border-transparent`.
- Active trigger: `text-(--color-ink) font-semibold border-(--color-ink)` — the `border-b-2` overlapping the list's `border-b` produces a clean underline.
- Hover (inactive): `text-(--color-ink)` (no border change).
- Caveat: if shadcn-vue's Tabs primitives don't expose `class` overrides per trigger, fall back to wrapping with class bindings inside `ExtTabs.vue` only.

### Commit 13 — `feat(ext-detail): enrich Related list with badge and dept`

**File:** `server/repositories/extensions.ts`

- Extend `relatedSelect` (line 81) to include three more columns: `badge: extensions.badge`, `deptId: extensions.deptId`, `starsAvg: extensions.starsAvg`. No SQL change — just adding to the select projection. `downloadsCount` is already there.

**File:** `app/components/extension/ExtRelatedList.vue`

- Update the `RelatedRow` interface to match the enriched shape (add `badge`, `deptId`, `starsAvg`).
- Each row becomes a tiny mini-card: emoji + name on row 1, `dept · ★ rating` muted meta on row 2. Optional badge pill inline with the name.
- Keep the row clickable as a whole via the `NuxtLink`.

## Files modified

**Edited:**

- `app/pages/extensions/[slug].vue` — commit 9 (breadcrumb)
- `app/components/extension/ExtHero.vue` — commit 9
- `app/components/extension/InstallButton.vue` — commit 9
- `app/components/extension/ExtTabs.vue` — commits 10, 12
- `app/components/extension/ExtAboutCard.vue` — commit 11
- `app/components/extension/ExtRelatedList.vue` — commits 11, 13
- `server/repositories/extensions.ts` — commit 13
- `i18n/locales/en.json`, `i18n/locales/zh.json` — commit 9

**Possibly edited (if Tabs primitives need override):**

- `app/components/ui/TabsList.vue`, `app/components/ui/TabsTrigger.vue` — commit 12 (only if shadcn-vue Tabs primitives can't accept custom classes from the parent)

**Untouched (verified still right):**

- `app/components/extension/SaveButton.vue` — size bump applied via class only, no component change needed
- `app/components/extension/ShareButton.vue` — same
- `app/components/extension/InstallCommand.vue` — keeps current terminal look; lives inside the new framed Setup tab
- `app/components/Markdown.vue` — keeps current prose render; the new card frame is in ExtTabs

## Reused utilities

- `useLocalePath()` — for the breadcrumb's locale-aware link
- `deptPath()` from `shared/data/departments.ts` — for the new Related row meta
- CSS vars (`--color-ink`, `--color-ink-muted`, `--color-border`, `--color-accent`, `--color-card`, `--radius-card`) — unchanged
- `badge-official` / `badge-popular` / `badge-new` classes from `tailwind.css` — reused in Related rows
- Existing `Tabs` primitive set (`Tabs`, `TabsList`, `TabsTrigger`, `TabsContent`) — kept unless commit 12 forces a primitive override

## Accent budget (decision #11)

The detail page is one of the two surfaces explicitly allowed an accent CTA. After this pass:

- ✅ Install button: now `size="lg"` accent CTA — the page's intentional accent moment, more prominent
- ✅ SaveButton active-state border + bg (existing)
- ✅ Permissions list dots (existing accent dots)
- ✅ "Mine" dept emphasis (existing)
- ❌ Tabs active state: now ink underline, not accent — keeps the budget tight

The hero now carries one bold accent CTA instead of three muted same-size buttons, which fits the "one intentional accent moment" goal better than today.

## Verification

1. **Local dev**: `bun dev` → open `http://localhost:3001/en/extensions/<any-slug>`.
2. **Breadcrumb**: a `← Browse extensions` link sits above the hero; clicking returns to `/en/extensions`.
3. **Hero**: title reads at `text-4xl` serif; description is a noticeably larger lede; emoji is size-16; stats row uses middle-dot separators throughout.
4. **CTA group**: Install reads as the primary action (larger, accent fill); Save + Share match in size but stay neutral.
5. **README card**: Overview tab content lives inside a card with border + p-6; tags appear at the card's bottom with a divider above them; Setup and Permissions tabs use the same card frame.
6. **Sidebar**: "About" and "Related" headers are serif text-base; cards have p-6 breathing room; Related rows show `dept · ★ rating` under the name.
7. **Tabs**: active tab has a clean ink underline; inactive triggers are muted ink that goes ink on hover; the `bg-(--color-sidebar)` container is gone.
8. **Locale parity**: switch to `/zh/extensions/<slug>` — breadcrumb and all new copy renders in Chinese.
9. **Theme parity**: cycle Ivory → Dark → Mono Clean. Card frames around tab content should be visible in all three; tab underline should remain legible in Dark (ink is bright in dark mode).
10. **Validate**: `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test`.

## Out of scope

- Versions panel / version history surface
- Screenshots gallery
- Inline comments / reviews
- Detail-page error and loading skeletons (separate concern from the list skeleton)
- TopBar / Sidebar changes
- Pagination / discovery beyond the Related sidebar

## Prior work on this branch (reference)

- C1 `9adb8be` refactor(ext-list): tighten ExtCard hierarchy and typography
- C2 `cff0aea` feat(ext-list): add ResultsSummary with active filter chips
- C3 `3fee07a` refactor(ext-list): relocate sort to ResultsSummary, soften FilterBar
- C4 `08a1b5c` feat(ext-list): add ExtGridSkeleton for first paint
- C5 `724d183` refactor(ext-list): simplify ExtCard to a preview rectangle
- C6 `93ba9be` refactor(ext-list): quiet FilterBar to a single pill row
- C7 `b938ebb` refactor(ext-list): drop FilterChips component
- C8 `e01ef18` refactor(ext-list): roomier ExtCard and grid
- (also `368f0b7` chore(entire): ignore redactors/local/)

```

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Bookmark, BookmarkCheck } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	const props = defineProps<{
5	  extensionId: string
6	  variant?: "pill" | "icon"
7	}>()
8	
9	const { t } = useI18n()
10	const router = useRouter()
11	const localePath = useLocalePath()
12	
13	const saved = ref(false)
14	const busy = ref(false)
15	let resetTimer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
16	
17	async function onClick() {
18	  if (busy.value) return
19	  busy.value = true
20	  try {
21	    await $fetch("/api/internal/installs", {
22	      method: "POST",
23	      body: { extensionId: props.extensionId },
24	    })
25	    saved.value = true
26	    if (resetTimer) clearTimeout(resetTimer)
27	    resetTimer = setTimeout(() => { saved.value = false }, 3000)
28	  } catch (err) {
29	    const statusCode = (err as { statusCode?: number })?.statusCode
30	    if (statusCode === 401) {
31	      await router.push(localePath("/sign-in"))
32	      return
33	    }
34	    console.error("[save] failed", err)
35	  } finally {
36	    busy.value = false
37	  }
38	}
39	
40	onBeforeUnmount(() => { if (resetTimer) clearTimeout(resetTimer) })
41	</script>
42	
43	<template>
44	  <button
45	    type="button"
46	    :disabled="busy"
47	    :aria-label="saved ? t('common.saved') : t('common.save')"
48	    class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border px-3 py-1.5 text-sm transition-colors disabled:opacity-60"
49	    :class="saved
50	      ? 'border-(--color-accent) bg-(--color-accent)/10 text-(--color-accent)'
51	      : 'border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)'"
52	    @click="onClick"
53	  >
54	    <component :is="saved ? BookmarkCheck : Bookmark" :size="14" aria-hidden="true" />
55	    <span v-if="variant !== 'icon'">{{ saved ? t("common.saved") : t("common.save") }}</span>
56	  </button>
57	</template>
58

1	<script setup lang="ts">
2	import { Share2 } from "lucide-vue-next"
3	
4	const props = defineProps<{ url: string }>()
5	
6	const { t } = useI18n()
7	const copied = ref(false)
8	let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null
9	
10	async function share() {
11	  // Prefer the native share sheet (iOS / Android / some desktop browsers).
12	  if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && typeof navigator.share === "function") {
13	    try {
14	      await navigator.share({ url: props.url })
15	      return
16	    } catch (err) {
17	      // User dismissed → don't silently copy as a consolation prize, that's
18	      // surprising. Only fall through on genuine errors (e.g. share blocked
19	      // by permissions policy on some embedded contexts).
20	      if ((err as { name?: string })?.name === "AbortError") return
21	    }
22	  }
23	  if (typeof navigator !== "undefined" && navigator.clipboard) {
24	    try {
25	      await navigator.clipboard.writeText(props.url)
26	      copied.value = true
27	      if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
28	      timer = setTimeout(() => { copied.value = false }, 1400)
29	    } catch {
30	      // ignore — clipboard permissions vary
31	    }
32	  }
33	}
34	
35	onBeforeUnmount(() => { if (timer) clearTimeout(timer) })
36	</script>
37	
38	<template>
39	  <button
40	    type="button"
41	    class="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 rounded-md border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) px-3 py-1.5 text-sm text-(--color-ink) hover:bg-(--color-sidebar)"
42	    @click="share"
43	  >
44	    <Share2 :size="14" aria-hidden="true" />
45	    {{ copied ? t("extensions.copied") : t("extensions.share") }}
46	  </button>
47	</template>
48	
```

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport type { Locale } from \"~~/shared/types\"\nimport { tagLabel } from \"~~/shared/tags\"\n\nconst props = defineProps<{\n  readmeMd: string | null\n  tagIds: string[]\n  slug: string\n  permissions: Record<string, unknown> | null\n}>()\n\nconst { t, locale } = useI18n()\n\nconst tab = ref<\"overview\" | \"setup\" | \"permissions\">(\"overview\")\n\nconst installCommand = computed(() => `agentcenter install ${props.slug}`)\n\nconst permissionEntries = computed(() => {\n  if (!props.permissions) return []\n  return Object.entries(props.permissions)\n    .filter(([, v]) => Boolean(v))\n    .map(([k]) => k)\n})\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <Tabs v-model=\"tab\" default-value=\"overview\">\n    <TabsList>\n      <TabsTrigger value=\"overview\">{{ t(\"extensions.tabs.overview\") }}</TabsTrigger>\n      <TabsTrigger value=\"setup\">{{ t(\"extensions.tabs.setup\") }}</TabsTrigger>\n      <TabsTrigger value=\"permissions\">{{ t(\"extensions.tabs.permissions\") }}</TabsTrigger>\n    </TabsList>\n\n    <TabsContent value=\"overview\">\n      <div\n        class=\"mt-4 rounded-(--radius-card) border t(\"extensions.noReadme\") }}</p>\n        <div\n          v-if=\"tagIds.length > 0\"\n          class=\"mt-6 flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 border-t border-(--color-border) pt-4\"\n        >\n          <span\n            v-for=\"tag in tagIds\"\n            :key=\"tag\"\n            class=\"border border-(--color-border) text-(--color-ink-muted) rounded px-2 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] font-semibold\"\n          >\n            #{{ tagLabel(tag, locale as Locale) }}\n          </span>\n        </div>\n      </div>\n    </TabsContent>\n\n    <TabsContent value=\"setup\">\n      <div\n        class=\"mt-4 rounded-(--radius-card) border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-6\"\n      >\n        <p class=\"mb-3 text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n          {{ t(\"extensions.setupHint\") }}\n        </p>\n        <InstallCommand :command=\"installCommand\" />\n      </div>\n    </TabsContent>\n\n    <TabsContent value=\"permissions\">\n      <div\n        class=\"mt-4 rounded-(--radius-card) border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-6\"\n      >\n        <ul\n          v-if=\"permissionEntries.length > 0\"\n          class=\"space-y-1.5 text-sm\"\n        >\n          <li\n            v-for=\"key in permissionEntries\"\n            :key=\"key\"\n            class=\"flex items-center gap-2 text-(--color-ink)\"\n          >\n            <span class=\"size-1.5 rounded-full bg-(--color-accent)\" />\n            {{ t(`publish.wizard.listing.permissions.${key}`, key) }}\n          </li>\n        </ul>\n        <p v-else class=\"text-sm text-(--color-ink-muted)\">\n          {{ t(\"extensions.noPermissions\") }}\n        </p>\n      </div>\n    </TabsContent>\n  </Tabs>\n</template>\n"

  "content": "<script setup lang=\"ts\">\nimport { Star } from \"lucide-vue-next\"\nimport { deptPath } from \"~~/shared/data/departments\"\nimport type { Locale } from \"~~/shared/types\"\n\ninterface RelatedRow {\n  id: string\n  slug: string\n  name: string\n  nameZh?: string | null\n  iconEmoji: string | null\n  iconColor: string | null\n  downloadsCount?: number\n  badge?: \"official\" | \"popular\" | \"new\" | null\n  deptId?: string | null\n  starsAvg?: string | null\n}\n\nconst props = defineProps<{ related: RelatedRow[] }>()\n\nconst { t, locale } = useI18n()\nconst localePath = useLocalePath()\n\nconst BADGE_LABEL = { official: \"Official\", popular: \"Popular\", new: \"New\" } as const\nconst BADGE_CLASS = {\n  official: \"badge-official\",\n  popular: \"badge-popular\",\n  new: \"badge-new\",\n} as const\n\nfunction labelFor(r: RelatedRow): string {\n  if (locale.value === \"zh\" && r.nameZh) return r.nameZh\n  return r.name\n}\n\nfunction deptLeafFor(r: RelatedRow): string | null {\n  if (!r.deptId) return null\n  const trail = deptPath(r.deptId, locale.value as Locale)\n  return trail.length > 0 ? (trail[trail.length - 1] ?? null) : null\n}\n\nfunction ratingFor(r: RelatedRow): string | null {\n  if (!r.starsAvg) return null\n  const n = Number(r.starsAvg)\n  if (Number.isNaN(n) || n <= 0) return null\n  return n.toFixed(1)\n}\n</script>\n\n<template>\n  <div\n    v-if=\"props.related.length > 0\"\n    class=\"rounded-(--radius-card) border border-(--color-border) bg-(--color-card) p-6\"\n  >\n    <h2 class=\"font-serif text-base font-semibold tracking-tight mb-4 text-(--color-ink)\">\n      {{ t(\"extensions.related\") }}\n    </h2>\n    <ul class=\"space-y-3\">\n      <li v-for=\"r in props.related\" :key=\"r.id\">\n        <NuxtLink\n          :to=\"localePath(`/extensions/${r.slug}`)\"\n          class=\"group flex items-start gap-3\"\n        >\n          <span class=\"shrink-0 text-[18px] leading-none mt-0.5\">{{ r.iconEmoji }}</span>\n          <div class=\"min-w-0 flex-1\">\n            <div class=\"flex items-center gap-1.5\">\n              <span class=\"truncate text-[13px] font-medium text-(--color-ink) group-hover:text-(--color-accent) transition-colors\">\n                {{ labelFor(r) }}\n              </span>\n              <span\n                v-if=\"r.badge\"\n                class=\"shrink-0 rounded px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[10px] font-semibold\"\n                :class=\"BADGE_CLASS[r.badge]\"\n              >\n                {{ BADGE_LABEL[r.badge] }}\n              </span>\n            </div>\n            <div\n              v-if=\"deptLeafFor(r) || ratingFor(r)\"\n              class=\"mt-0.5 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-[11px] text-(--color-ink-muted)\"\n            >\n              <span v-if=\"deptLeafFor(r)\" class=\"truncate\">{{ deptLeafFor(r) }}</span>\n              <span\n                v-if=\"deptLeafFor(r) && ratingFor(r)\"\n                aria-hidden=\"true\"\n                class=\"text-(--color-ink-muted)/50\"\n              >·</span>\n              <span v-if=\"ratingFor(r)\" class=\"inline-flex items-center gap-0.5\">\n                <Star :size=\"10\" class=\"fill-amber-500 text-amber-500\" aria-hidden=\"true\" />\n                <span class=\"font-mono\">{{ ratingFor(r) }}</span>\n              </span>\n            </div>\n          </div>\n        </NuxtLink>\n      </li>\n    </ul>\n  </div>\n</template>\n"
}

Branch `feat/ext-list-elegance` now has **13 elegance commits + 1 chore**. The five new detail-page commits:

- **`feat(ext-detail): amplify hero with breadcrumb and size=lg CTA`** (732d6be) — breadcrumb above hero, title to `text-4xl serif`, true lede at `text-[17px]`, icon to size-16, proper `·` separators on stats, new `InstallButton size="lg"`, matching Save/Share sizing.

- **`refactor(ext-detail): editorial sidebar pass`** (d63bd19) — About/Related h2s become `font-serif text-base tracking-tight`; cards bump to `p-6`; body shifts to `text-[13px]` with mono on license + date.

- **`feat(ext-detail): enrich Related list with badge, dept, and stars`** (ac96745) — three more columns added to `relatedSelect`; each row renders badge inline + `dept · ★ rating` meta line below the name.

Each `SectorCard` / `DomainCard` now has a single-line header — cleaner hierarchy, lets the MCP tiles below own the per-group color story.\n- Soften ZH copy `无需 MCP` → `暂无 MCP 诉求` (and short form `无需` → `暂无`). Reads as a tentative \"no demand yet\" rather than a hard verdict, matching how PMs actually talk about products that haven't surfaced an MCP need.\n- Replace ZH `行业` with `产业` across the panorama (layer label, sidebar section, home feature blurb). Broader term the rest of the org uses for the industry layer.\n\nEN copy and the `Layer = \"industry\" | \"public\"` TS/DB enums are unchanged — only the ZH display strings shift.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] `/zh/mcp-panorama` — sector + domain cards have no thin colored bar; `X/Y 已发布` counter still on the right.\n- [ ] Layer toggle, sidebar header, and `industryDesc` all read `产业` / `产业服务` / `面向各产业的工具服务`.\n- [ ] With a status filter applied so some groups are empty, the page subtitle and `LayerSummary` \"No MCP\" stat box both read `暂无 MCP 诉求`.\n- [ ] `/en/mcp-panorama` — English strings unchanged.\n- [ ] `bun run lint` clean; `bun run typecheck` clean; `app/components/mcp-landscape/` tests pass.\n\n\u003c!-- This is an auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai --\u003e\n## Summary by CodeRabbit\n\n* **UI Updates**\n  * Simplified card header: removed the status legend/percentage display to present a cleaner header with the drill-in control and release count/total, reducing visual clutter and focusing on primary actions.\n\n* **Localization**\n  * Updated Chinese copy for the MCP panorama: refined terminology and status messaging (e.g., industry/sector labels and “no MCP” phrasing) for clearer, more consistent user-facing language.\n\n\u003c!-- review_stack_entry_start --\u003e\n\n[![Review Change Stack](https://storage.googleapis.com/coderabbit_public_assets/review-stack-in-coderabbit-ui.svg)](https://app.coderabbit.ai/change-stack/ainaive/agentcenter-nuxt/pull/26?utm_source=github_walkthrough\u0026utm_medium=github\u0026utm_campaign=change_stack)\n\n\u003c!-- review_stack_entry_end --\u003e\n\u003c!-- end of auto-generated comment: release notes by coderabbit.ai --\u003e","title":"refactor(mcp-panorama): elegance pass — drop per-card bar, soften ZH copy"}

Same product, same data, same i18n — just lighter chrome, more deliberate typography, and a clearer hierarchy.\n\n**Extensions list** (`/en/extensions`)\n- ExtCard: simplified to a preview rectangle — tag pills and inline Install button removed, dept/stars/downloads consolidated onto a single bullet-separated meta line. Wider, shorter card with 3-line description; min grid width 320px so cards settle into 3 columns at standard desktop.\n- FilterBar: hard `border-b` divider gone; collapsed to a single quiet pill row; ScopePills active state lightened from full-ink inversion to `bg-(--color-ink)/8` tinted outline; picker triggers drop the `Department:`/`Creator:`/`Publisher:` prefixes (the active selection or category name speaks for itself); FilterChips quick-filter pivots (Trending/New/Official/Open Source) dropped entirely — they duplicated sort and the visible Official badge.\n- New `ResultsSummary` band between filters and grid: count + dismissible chips per active filter + `Clear all` link + sort dropdown.\n- New `ExtGridSkeleton` for first paint.\n\n**Extensions detail** (`/en/extensions/[slug]`)\n- Breadcrumb back to `/extensions` above the hero so deep-linked detail pages have a clear return path.\n- Hero amplified: title bumps to `text-4xl` serif, description becomes a true lede (`text-[17px]`), icon grows to size-16, and the stats row gets proper `·` separators instead of an ASCII period. New `InstallButton size=\\\"lg\\\"` variant makes the primary CTA read as primary; Save and Share match in size but stay neutral.\n- Every tab content (`Overview`, `Setup`, `Permissions`) wrapped in a card div — the README finally has the editorial frame the sidebar already had.\n- Sidebar editorial pass: `About` and `Related` headers shift to `font-serif text-base tracking-tight`; cards bump to `p-6`; body to `text-[13px]` with mono on license + date.\n- Tabs switched from a timid background swap (1.5% lightness shift) to a true underline-on-active design — `border-b-2 border-(--color-ink)` on the active trigger.\n- Related list enriched: each row now shows badge inline with name + a muted `dept · ★ rating` meta line below. Three more columns (`badge`, `deptId`, `starsAvg`) added to the existing `relatedSelect` projection — no new SQL.\n\n**Accent budget (product decision #11)**\n- List page: Install button gone from cards → fewer accent moments per viewport. ResultsSummary chips and ScopePills active state are deliberately neutral.\n- Detail page: hero now carries one bold `size=\\\"lg\\\"` accent CTA instead of three muted same-size buttons. Tabs use ink underline, not accent.\n\n## Test plan\n\n- [ ] `bun run lint && bun run typecheck && bun run test` clean.\n- [ ] `/en/extensions` — cards show emoji + name + badge / 3-line description / `dept · ★ rating · ⬇ downloads`. No tag pills, no inline Install. 3 columns at standard desktop width.\n- [ ] Filter rail is a single line of compact pills; no horizontal rule below; no `Trending/New/Official/Open Source` row.\n- [ ] Apply scope + dept + tag filters — `ResultsSummary` shows count + dismissible chips + `Clear all`; clicking the X on a chip removes only that filter; `Clear all` drops to `/extensions`.\n- [ ] `/en/extensions/<slug>` — `← Browse extensions` breadcrumb returns to the list. Hero title is `text-4xl` serif; CTA trio reads as Install primary + Save/Share neutral, all matching size.\n- [ ] Overview/Setup/Permissions tabs each render inside a card frame; Overview tags appear at the card's bottom under a divider.\n- [ ] Active tab has a clean ink underline; inactive triggers go ink on hover. No `bg-(--color-sidebar)` container.\n- [ ] Sidebar `About` and `Related` headers are serif; cards have visible breathing room; Related rows show badge + `dept · ★ rating`.\n- [ ] `/zh/extensions` and `/zh/extensions/<slug>` render Chinese copy for the new `backToBrowse` and `summary.*` keys.\n- [ ] Cycle Ivory → Dark → Mono Clean — all three themes render the new chrome legibly.\n- [ ] `?filter=trending` (legacy URL) still loads cleanly and surfaces as a dismissible chip in ResultsSummary.\nEOF\n)\" 2>&1 | tail -5",

  _⚠️ Potential issue_ | _🟠 Major_ | _⚡ Quick win_

## CI / mergeability

## CodeRabbit's 8 inline comments

**Substantive — worth addressing:**

| # | File:line | Issue |
|---|-----------|-------|
| 1 | `ExtGrid.vue:40` | `minmax(320px,1fr)` can overflow on viewports <320px. Suggested `grid-cols-1 sm:grid-cols-[repeat(auto-fill,minmax(320px,1fr))]`. |
| 2 | `ExtCard.vue:99` | `Number(ext.starsAvg).toFixed(1)` renders `NaN` if `starsAvg` is null. Add a `"—"` fallback. |
| 3 | `ExtRelatedList.vue:24` | `BADGE_LABEL` is hardcoded English (`{official: "Official", …}`). ZH users see mixed-language chips. (`ExtCard.vue` has the same pre-existing pattern.) |

**Accent-budget concerns — debatable:**

| # | File:line | Issue |
|---|-----------|-------|
| 4 | `ExtAboutCard.vue:64,74` | Homepage/repo links use `hover:text-(--color-accent)`. Pre-existing — I didn't add these. |
| 5 | `ExtCard.vue:66` | Card uses `hover:text-(--color-accent)` on name + `hover:border-(--color-accent)/30`. I called the border hover out as an allowed accent moment in the plan. |
| 6 | `ExtRelatedList.vue:67` | Related row hover uses `group-hover:text-(--color-accent)`. Added in C13. |
| 7 | `ExtTabs.vue:79` | Permission list bullet dot uses `bg-(--color-accent)`. Pre-existing. |

CodeRabbit is reading decision #11 maximally — every accent appearance becomes "consumes budget." Reading more leniently: hover-only text shifts on link-like elements aren't the same as the **filled** active states the decision targets ("tinted fills"), and the permission dot has been there all along. None of these introduce new accent moments that weren't already in the codebase.

**Process flag — needs your call:**

| # | File:line | Issue |
|---|-----------|-------|
| 8 | `FilterBar.vue:17` | CodeRabbit cites CLAUDE.md decision #3: "Implement filter UI using Mode B (drawer-style multi-row) only." Round 2 collapsed FilterBar to a single quiet pill row at your direction. Per CLAUDE.md: *"If a PR needs to change one, update this section and `docs/plan.md` in the same commit as the code change."* I didn't update decision #3 when shipping C6. |

## What I'd recommend

- Fix #1, #2, #3 in a small follow-up commit on this branch.
- Update CLAUDE.md decision #3 (and `docs/plan.md` if it echoes Mode B) to reflect the new single-row direction — addresses #8 the correct way.
- Leave #4–#7 as-is unless you want to tighten further; they're all hover/decoration on long-standing patterns. Worth a reply on the PR explaining the call so the constraint is documented for future reviews.

Want me to ship the fixes + decision update?

```
25	
26	These mirror the original v1 product decisions. Same numbering; do not revisit inside a normal PR. If a PR needs to change one, update this section and `docs/plan.md` in the same commit as the code change.
27	
28	1. **Multi-tenant schema, single-tenant UI** for v1.
29	2. **Themes**: Editorial Ivory (default) + Dark + Mono Clean. Selectable via the TopBar theme picker; persisted in a `theme` cookie.
30	3. **Filter UI**: Mode B (drawer-style multi-row) only.
31	4. **Sign-up**: open. SSO/invite-only later if needed.
32	5. **Locale URLs**: always prefixed; no implicit default.
33	6. **Detail page README**: raw markdown stored, server-rendered with markdown-it + DOMPurify. Manifest metadata (homepage, repo, license, compatibility, screenshots) rendered as a side panel.
34	7. **Install button**: triggers real install via the Agent CLI. Web records install events; the CLI does the work.
35	8. **Agent CLI**: agent-agnostic with Claude-first defaults. Extensions declare destination paths per agent in their manifest; user can override via `agentcenter config set`. Default target: `~/.claude/...`. Distributed via npm.
36	9. **Department IDs**: dotted-path text PKs (e.g. `eng.cloud.infra`). Descendant filter is a single `LIKE 'parent.%'` predicate, not a recursive CTE.
37	10. **Dynamic content i18n**: column-per-language (`name` + `nameZh`, `description` + `descriptionZh`). `tags` table carries `labelEn` + `labelZh`.
38	11. **Accent usage**: `--color-accent` is reserved for primary CTAs, focus rings, the home featured hero, and the active extension-detail install action. Secondary active states (sidebar nav, filter trigger pills, non-primary picker chips) use neutral ink + left-rule or bold weight, not tinted fills. The home should carry at most two accent moments per viewport; the goal is "one intentional accent moment" wherever practical. Auth pages, error states, and UI primitive focus rings are exempt.
39	
```

=== docs/daily-log.md (head 80) ===
# AgentCenter Daily Log · 每日简报

What changed each day. For the current capability list, see [features.md](./features.md).

每日变更记录。当前已交付能力清单见 [features.md](./features.md)。

> Note: this log starts fresh for the Nuxt rewrite. The original Next.js daily entries live in the archived repo; the product surface they describe is reproduced verbatim in [features.md](./features.md).

> 说明：本日志针对 Nuxt 重写从头开始记录。原 Next.js 仓库的历史每日记录保留在归档仓库；其描述的产品面已在 [features.md](./features.md) 中完整复刻。

---

## English

### 2026-05-19

**Briefing.** Today's release reshapes the MCP Panorama around realistic multi-MCP tools — a software tool can now expose several MCP servers — and strips editorial labels from every group's stats in favour of the raw numbers, all of which double as one-click drill and filter actions.

<details>
<summary>Details</summary>

- **A software tool can ship multiple MCPs** — A tool no longer collapses to one MCP listing. CodeCheck now exposes both codecheck-mcp and molint-mcp; K8sOps and ObservHub each ship three. The detail panel adds an "Other MCPs in this tool" switcher so you can jump between sibling MCPs without closing the panel. (#24)

- **A more realistic MCP inventory** — Every MCP carries a descriptive name (kubectl-mcp, helm-mcp, metrics-mcp, identity-mcp, …) instead of a generic "tool-name-mcp" placeholder. The pool of "no MCP needed" tools shrinks from 19 to 6 truly legacy or manual workflows, so the panorama reflects an organization that actually ships MCPs. (#24)

- **Tool-as-card layout** — Each software tool reads as a small quiet card with a serif name and a small status dot; its MCP servers render inside as vivid status-coloured pills. PDTs become subtly recessed regions, giving the page a clear hierarchy: domain → recessed PDT → raised tool card → vivid MCP pills. (#24)

- **Raw numbers replace ranking labels** — Group headers no longer carry a "Leading / On track / Lagging" verdict pill. Each shows the underlying numbers — total count, a "5/8 released" fraction, and a thin stacked status bar. The summary's right block lists the top three groups by release %, ranked numerically, instead of singling out one "Leading" name. (#25)

- **Every count is now clickable** — Numbers on the page double as actions. Click a group's title to drill in; click "Released 87" to flip the status filter to released (and again to flip back to all); click a top-three entry to jump straight there. (#25)

- **Developer ergonomics in the detail panel** — A copy-MCP-URI button sits next to the endpoint for released MCPs, with a check-icon confirmation. The MCP's tags surface as small chips above the metadata grid, so you can see at a glance what an MCP covers. (#25)

</details>

---

### 2026-05-18

**Briefing.** Today's release pivots browsing toward type-first navigation, gives the home page a real curated "Featured this week" spotlight, and adds a Mono Clean theme alongside Ivory and Dark.

<details>
<summary>Details</summary>

- **Mono Clean theme + 3-way switcher** — A third theme joins Editorial Ivory and Dark. The old toggle becomes a labelled dropdown listing all three options with short descriptors and preview tiles, so the choice and what each looks like sit side by side. (#22)

- **"Featured this week" on the home page** — The home banner replaces the generic placeholder with a real curated extension: name, tagline, install command, and a deep link to the detail page. When no extension is marked featured, the home falls back to a slim editorial card. (#23)

- **Type-first Explore dropdown** — Clicking Explore in the top bar now opens a dropdown listing the four extension types (Skills, MCP Servers, Slash Commands, Plugins) plus MCP Panorama. Browsing always starts with a type choice; the flat "all extensions" list is no longer the default destination but stays reachable as a fallback. (#23)

- **Sidebar leads with the function-types taxonomy** — Enterprise users who navigate by functional domain now see the full Work Task / Business / Tools tree as their primary sidebar navigation. Browse-by-type pills sit above; the placeholder Collections section is hidden until it carries real data. (#23)

- **Quieter editorial shell** — Sidebar and main content share one background with no dividing line, the top bar is taller, the search bar is borderless until focus, the sidebar's vertical scrollbar is hidden, and the accent color is reserved for the home spotlight and primary CTAs — secondary states use weight or a thin left rule instead. (#23)

</details>

---

### 2026-05-14

**Briefing.** A new MCP Panorama lets stakeholders see at a glance which internal tools have shipped an MCP version, which are in development, and which don't need one — and production deploys now keep their database in sync automatically.

<details>
<summary>Details</summary>

- **MCP Panorama landscape page** — A new bilingual page at `/mcp-panorama` maps every internal tool service onto one screen, grouped by industry sector (Wireless, Datacom, Cloud, Terminals, and nine others) or by public-services domain → PDT (AI R&D, Product & Software, Hardware, Product Digitization, Infrastructure). Each tile is colour-coded by status — green for MCP released, amber for in development, grey for no MCP needed — and the top of each group shows a coverage bar plus a Leading / On track / Lagging / Early tag so leading and lagging units pop out. A right-side panel opens on click with status detail, dependent count, owner team, MCP address, and downstream consumers. Green tiles link straight to the marketplace listing for that MCP. A grouped-list layout is offered as an alternate view. (#17)

- **Contextual catalog entry** — Filtering the extensions catalog to MCP Servers now surfaces a "View Panorama →" banner above the grid; the top-bar nav also keeps a persistent "MCP Panorama" link. The earlier sidebar callout, which appeared on every page regardless of context, has been removed. (#18)

- **Self-syncing deploys** — Every production deploy now applies pending database changes before going live, so a feature that depends on a schema update works end-to-end on day one without a separate operator step. (#19)

</details>

---

### 2026-05-13

**Briefing.** The Nuxt rewrite reaches feature parity with the original Next.js implementation — the marketplace, publish wizard, profile workspace, CLI, and bilingual content all behave the same as before, just running on Nuxt 4.

<details>
